Result: 在GSO-30上，GeoFuse-MV3D相比MV-SAM3D基线降低CD 2.20%和LPIPS 2.02%，提升PSNR 2.36%和SSIM 1.03%。KnowledgeBank在Terminal-Bench上提升SR 4.53%、降低AS 4.95%，在SWE-Bench上提升resolve rate 3.73%、降低AS 5.65%。

Conclusion: 提出的两项改进有效解决了GeneralVLA中的3D重建幻觉和记忆管理问题，在多个基准上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Generalist vision-language-action systems need object-centric 3D evidence and reusable manipulation experience to plan reliable robot trajectories. GeneralVLA provides a hierarchical interface for converting language and RGB-D observations into 3D end-effector paths, but two bottlenecks remain. First, monocular SAM3D-style object reconstruction can hallucinate pose and unseen geometry, while manipulation benefits from stable object shape when calibrated multi-view observations are available. Second, the original KnowledgeBank mainly retrieves semantically similar snippets and appends new knowledge, which makes it difficult to control memory quality, conflicts, confidence, and geometric relevance. To address the first challenge, we introduce GeoFuse-MV3D, a geometry-prior-guided MV-SAM3D reconstruction branch that verifies external geometry cues with input-view masks, applies soft visual-hull support, performs axis-wise refinement, and fuses only geometry while preserving appearance. To address the second challenge, we upgrade KnowledgeBank into a governed long-term memory system with explicit quality, confidence, lifecycle, verifier, and conflict metadata, together with precision-oriented retrieval. Finally, we evaluate the reconstruction branch on GSO-30 and the memory module on Terminal-Bench 2.0 and SWE-Bench Verified; GeoFuse-MV3D improves over the MV-SAM3D baseline by reducing CD and LPIPS by 2.20% and 2.02% while increasing PSNR and SSIM by 2.36% and 1.03%, and KnowledgeBank improves over ReasoningBank by 4.53% on Terminal-Bench SR and 3.73% on SWE-Bench resolve rate, while reducing AS by 4.95% and 5.65%, respectively. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/GeneralVLA-2. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/GeneralVLA-2.

</details>


### [36] [SPHINX: First Explain, Then Explore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17482)
*Nguyen Do,Tue M. Cao,Tien Van Do,András Hajdu,Tamás Bérczes,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPHINX提出了一种“先解释，后探索”的闭环对抗场景生成框架，通过可解释AI分析驾驶策略的失败模式（如犹豫不决、多帧不一致），再利用视觉语言模型进行推理和批判，生成针对性的对抗场景以提升自动驾驶策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗驾驶场景生成方法（如ChatScene、LLM-Attacker）主要依赖大语言模型和视觉语言模型的先验知识，未能基于驾驶策略自身的失败诊断（如犹豫不决、多帧不一致）来针对性地生成场景，无法准确针对策略的弱点。

Method: 提出SPHINX闭环框架，遵循“先解释，后探索”原则：首先利用可解释人工智能方法分析策略，识别关键视觉概念及其对策略输出的影响以及决策不确定性；然后基于提取的可解释证据，使用视觉语言模型对当前策略的失败模式进行推理和批判；最后根据批判结果生成针对性的对抗场景，用于策略重训练和改进。

Result: SPHINX能够提供策略失败的可解释性说明，而其他对抗场景生成方法无法做到。在多个基准测试和测试套件中，SPHINX可适用于多种最先进的自动驾驶架构，并在鲁棒性提升方面持续优于现有场景生成方法。

Conclusion: 基于策略自身失败诊断的对抗场景生成比依赖先验知识的方法更有效，SPHINX框架通过可解释AI与VLM的结合，实现了对驾驶策略弱点的精准定位与针对性提升。

Abstract: Generating adversarial driving scenarios is critical for evaluating and improving autonomous vehicle decision-making systems in simulation. Recent approaches, such as ChatScene and LLM-Attacker, rely primarily on the prior knowledge of Large Language Models and Vision-Language Models to generate driving scenarios procedurally. We argue that adversarial scenes should be generated based on the failure diagnosis (e.g., indecisiveness, multi-frame inconsistency) of the driving policy to specifically address the policy's weaknesses instead of relying on prior assumptions. In this paper, we propose SPHINX, a closed-loop framework for adversarial scenario synthesis guided by a simple principle: first explain, then explore. Beyond blindly exploring the scenario space, SPHINX leverages explainable artificial intelligence methods to analyze the policy, identifying key visual concepts and their influence on policy outputs, and the uncertainty of the decisions. Given the interpretable evidence extracted from the policy's own decision process, we use a vision language model to rationalize and criticize failure modes of the current policy. These critics are then used to generate targeted adversarial scenarios for policy retraining and improvement. We demonstrate that SPHINX can highlight an interpretable account of policy failures while other adversarial scene generation cannot. Across the evaluated benchmarks and test suites, SPHINX can be applied to diverse state-of-the-art autonomous vehicle architectures and yields consistent robustness improvements over existing scenario-generation methods.

</details>


### [37] [OmniDrive: An LLM-Choreographed Multi-Agent World Model with Unified Latent Co-Compression for Multi-View Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17536)
*Zijie Meng,Yufei Liu,Chengqian Ma,Zhiyu Li,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhua Nie,Bingcai Wei,Shuqin Chen,Weichen Xu,Jiquan Yuan,Miao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRIVE-CHOREO是一个基于LLM编排的多智能体世界模型，通过引入共享符号中间语（WorldScript）解决异构控制注入和后验跨视角融合问题，在nuScenes上实现了多视角一致性和BEV mAP的新SOTA（21.6），且纯合成数据训练的检测器在真实验证集上NDS提升+2.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶生成式世界模型面临两个未解决的关键张力：一是异构控制注入问题（自由形式语言、HD地图、轨迹和相机位姿处于不兼容的表示空间），二是后验跨视角融合问题（每相机潜变量无法编码全局3D几何）。作者将这两个问题归因于同一根源：缺乏一个在潜令牌级别对齐语言、几何和像素的共享符号中间语。

Method: 提出DRIVE-CHOREO，一个由LLM编排的多智能体世界模型。包含三个Qwen2.5-VL智能体：Director（解析用户意图为结构化WorldScript）、Cartographer（将意图转化为空间锚定的布局令牌）、Auditor（反馈跨视角批评作为辅助监督）。三者联合生成位置感知令牌序列，该序列通过视角-时间置换与多视角视频共同压缩，在3D VAE的卷积感受野内强制实现跨相机几何约束。

Result: 在nuScenes上，多视角一致性和BEV mAP达到21.6（新SOTA），FVD为45.7（具竞争力）。纯合成数据训练的检测器在真实验证集上NDS提升+2.4。

Conclusion: DRIVE-CHOREO通过LLM编排的多智能体架构和共享符号中间语（WorldScript），有效解决了异构控制注入和跨视角几何融合问题，验证了合成数据在自动驾驶下游任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Generative world models for autonomous driving face two unresolved tensions: heterogeneous control injection, where free-form language, HD-maps, trajectories, and camera poses reside in incompatible representational spaces, and post-hoc cross-view fusion, where per-camera latents fail to encode global 3-D geometry. We trace both to a single root cause: the absence of a shared symbolic interlingua aligning language, geometry, and pixels at the latent-token level. We present DRIVE-CHOREO, an LLM-choreographed multi-agent world model that recasts controllable multi-view video generation as latent choreography. Three Qwen2.5-VL agents - a Director parsing user intent into a structured WorldScript, a Cartographer grounding it into spatially-anchored layout tokens, and an Auditor feeding cross-view critiques back as auxiliary supervision - jointly author a single position-aware token sequence. This sequence is co-compressed with the multi-view video via a view-time permutation that enforces inter-camera geometry within the convolutional receptive field of a 3-D VAE. On nuScenes, DRIVE-CHOREO sets new state-of-the-art multi-view consistency and BEV mAP (21.6) with competitive FVD (45.7); a detector trained purely on our synthetic data gains +2.4 NDS on the real validation split, validating downstream utility.

</details>


### [38] [Reinforcing Dual-Path Reasoning in Spatial Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17539)
*Yatai Ji,An-Chieh Cheng,Yang Fu,Yukang Chen,Han Zhang,Zhaojing Yang,Wei Huang,Ka Chun Cheung,Song Han,Vidya Nariyambut Murali,Pavlo Molchanov,Jan Kautz,Simon See,Hongxu Yin,Ping Luo,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SR-REAL是一个双路径空间推理框架，通过强化学习为空间VLM配备了语言纯推理（LOR）和检测后推理（DTR）两种互补策略，在多种空间基准上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间VLM在几何感知方面取得进展，但复杂空间推理（需要多步推断深度、距离和场景关系）仍然困难；同时，不同类型的空间查询需要根本不同的策略（纯语言演绎推理 vs. 3D显式接地后的定量推理），亟需一个统一的框架来融合这两种互补路径。

Method: SR-REAL包含三个阶段：(1) 冷启动监督微调阶段，构建LOR和DTR的链式思维监督，并暴露region-to-3D接口；(2) 强化学习阶段，使用准确性和格式奖励优化策略模型，对DTR额外使用基于离散中心检测的奖励以细化几何对齐；(3) 双路径推理：LOR进行逐步语言演绎推理，DTR通过区域标记检测3D几何线索（中心或边界框）后进行几何推理。

Result: 在多种空间基准上：(i) 单个RL训练模型支持两种推理路径，DTR在区域感知任务中通过精确3D定位表现优异，LOR增强通用空间推理；(ii) 联合训练两条路径促进相互增强；(iii) 高质量混合冷启动数据对稳定RL优化至关重要；(iv) 模型无需逐任务调优即可跨数据集和领域泛化，LOR与DTR之间呈现正向迁移。

Conclusion: SR-REAL通过强化学习统一了语言推理与3D检测推理两条路径，验证了双路径协同训练在复杂空间推理任务中的有效性，展现了良好的泛化能力和正向迁移效果。

Abstract: Spatial VLMs have made substantial progress in geometric perception, yet complex spatial reasoning requiring multi-step inference over depth, distance, and scene relations remains challenging. Moreover, different spatial queries call for fundamentally different strategies: some are best addressed through purely linguistic, step-by-step deduction, while others require explicit 3D grounding before quantitative inference. We present Dual-Path Spatial Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Spatial VLMs (SR-REAL), a unified framework that equips a spatial VLM with two complementary reasoning paths: Language-Only Reasoning (LOR), which performs step-by-step linguistic deduction, and Detect-Then-Reason (DTR), which detects 3D geometric cues (e.g., centers or bounding boxes) via region tokens before explicit geometric inference. SR-REAL begins with a cold-start supervised fine-tuning stage that constructs LOR and DTR chain-of-thought supervision and exposes a region-to-3D interface, followed by RL that optimizes the policy model with accuracy and format rewards; for DTR, a discrete center-based detection reward further refines geometric alignment. Across diverse spatial benchmarks, SR-REAL significantly outperforms spatial VLM baselines: (i) a single RL-trained model supports both reasoning paths, with DTR excelling in region-aware tasks through precise 3D localization and LOR enhancing general spatial reasoning; (ii) jointly training both paths fosters mutual reinforcement; (iii) high-quality, blended cold-start data is crucial for stable RL optimization; and (iv) the model generalizes across datasets and domains without per-task tuning, demonstrating positive transfer between LOR and DTR.

</details>


### [39] [TaFD: Threat-Aware Frequency Decoupling for Adversarial Robustness against Heterogeneous Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17540)
*Mengda Xie,Yiling He,Meie Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Threat-aware Frequency Decoupling (TaFD)框架，通过频率域解耦优化解决多威胁鲁棒性中的梯度不兼容问题，在多个数据集和架构上平均鲁棒准确率提升约11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合对抗训练(JAT)在应对异构威胁（如ℓ_p有界攻击与语义攻击）时存在负迁移问题，根本原因在于不同威胁的优化梯度不兼容，亟需一种解耦优化策略。

Method: 首先通过一阶梯度分析形式化梯度不兼容问题，理论论证解耦优化的必要性；发现冲突威胁在频域具有可分离的谱特征。提出两阶段框架TaFD：(1) 通过无监督聚类攻击谱原型发现潜在威胁域，训练轻量分类器用于推理时威胁域识别；(2) Frequency-Conditional Convolution学习威胁域特定的谱掩码，将样本路由至对应专家，实现结构参数分离。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet三个数据集上，采用ResNet和MobileViT两种架构进行验证。相比现有JAT和频域基线方法，TaFD在异构攻击下实现更均衡的鲁棒性，平均鲁棒准确率提升约11%，同时保持领先的干净准确率。

Conclusion: 频域解耦范式能有效缓解多威胁鲁棒性中的优化冲突问题，为处理异构对抗威胁提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Multi-threat robustness remains a fundamental challenge in deep learning. Although joint adversarial training (JAT) is widely adopted, it suffers from negative transfer under heterogeneous threats, particularly between $\ell_p$-bounded and semantic attacks. Through first-order gradient analysis, we formalize this as gradient incompatibility and theoretically establish the necessity of decoupled optimization. We further reveal that these conflicting threats exhibit separable spectral characteristics in the frequency domain. Motivated by this observation, we propose Threat-aware Frequency Decoupling (TaFD), a two-stage defense framework that reformulates JAT as a frequency-domain divide-and-conquer paradigm. TaFD first discovers latent threat domains via unsupervised clustering of attack spectral prototypes and trains a lightweight classifier for inference-time threat domain identification. Conditioned on the prediction, TaFD employs a Frequency-Conditional Convolution that learns threat-domain-specific spectral masks and routes each sample to the corresponding expert, enforcing structural parameter separation and alleviating optimization conflicts.
  We validate TaFD on three representative image-classification benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet) and on two representative architectures (the convolutional ResNet and the hybrid-transformer MobileViT). Extensive results demonstrate that TaFD achieves more balanced robustness against heterogeneous attacks than existing JAT and frequency-domain baselines, improving average robust accuracy by approximately 11\% over the strongest baseline while maintaining leading clean accuracy.

</details>


### [40] [Universal Image Restoration via Internalized Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17557)
*Yu Guo,Zhengru Fang,Shengfeng He,Senkang Hu,Yihang Tao,Phone Lin,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoTIR 提出将 Chain-of-Thought 推理内化到单一图像恢复模型中，通过将图像恢复视为图像编辑的子任务并利用 Lagrangian 优化实现可微分的 CoT 式学习，避免了多轮恢复的高计算成本和退化间交互建模不足的问题。配套发布含 520 万样本的 CoTIR-Bench 基准，实验表明在感知质量和保真度上均优于现有统一模型和多轮恢复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一全模型在复杂混合退化下性能下降，而基于 Chain-of-Thought 的多轮恢复方法存在计算成本高以及步骤推理中退化间交互建模薄弱两大局限。

Method: 将图像恢复视为图像编辑的子任务，利用大规模预训练编辑模型作为优化起点；通过 Lagrange 优化将结构化的 CoT 式推理编码为可微目标函数，使单一模型内化 CoT 推理过程，无需串联专用恢复模块。

Result: 在 CoTIR-Bench 及真实复合退化场景中，CoTIR 在感知质量和保真度上均优于统一模型和多轮恢复方法。

Conclusion: 将 CoT 推理内化到单一模型结合预训练编辑模型，是应对复杂混合退化图像恢复的有效范式。

Abstract: Image restoration seeks to recover high-quality images from degraded inputs but becomes highly ill-posed under complex, mixed degradations. While unified all-in-one models are common, their performance declines as degradation complexity increases. Recent works adopt Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning for multi-round restoration using specialized modules. However, this approach faces two key limitations: (i) increased computational cost due to multi-step processing, and (ii) weak modeling of interactions between degradations during stepwise inference. We introduce CoTIR, a universal image restoration framework that internalizes CoT reasoning within a single model. Concretely, we view image restoration as a specialized subtask of image editing, which implies that a large-scale pre-trained editing model provides a more favorable optimization starting point. Building on this, we fine-tune the model for restoration and further encode structured CoT-style reasoning into the learning objective via a differentiable formulation inspired by Lagrangian optimization, enabling holistic restoration without chaining specialized restorers. To facilitate training and evaluation, we further present CoTIR-Bench, a large-scale benchmark comprising 5.2 million samples with CoT-style reasoning traces. Extensive experiments on CoTIR-Bench and broad real composite degradation scenes show that CoTIR achieves stronger perceptual quality and more competitive fidelity than both all-in-one models and multi-round restoration methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/gy65896/CoTIR.

</details>


### [41] [RT-Counter: Real-Time Text-Guided Open-Vocabulary Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17561)
*Hao-Yuan Ma,Li Zhang,Zhiwei Zhu,Jie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RT-Counter框架，通过视觉原型文本化(VPT)模块和Weaving Transformer(Weaformer)层，在开放词汇目标计数任务中同时实现了高精度(MAE 13.30)和高实时性(112.48 FPS)，比现有方法快7.4倍且参数效率提升4倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言预训练模型的开放词汇目标计数方法在细粒度空间理解和实时推理方面存在不足，难以同时满足高精度和实时性要求。

Method: 提出RT-Counter框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 视觉原型文本化(VPT)模块，将视觉特征投影到文本特征空间，生成兼具抽象信息和细节原型信息的特征；2) Weaving Transformer(Weaformer)层，采用混合注意力机制高效融合局部和全局视觉特征，大幅降低计算成本。

Result: 在FSC147数据集上MAE为13.30，推理速度达112.48 FPS，比现有领先方法快7.4倍，参数效率提升4倍以上。

Conclusion: RT-Counter成功打破了开放词汇目标计数任务中精度与速度的权衡，实现了高精度与实时性能的平衡。

Abstract: Text-guided open-vocabulary object counting (TOOC) aims to count objects belonging to the categories specified by natural language descriptions. Although vision-language pre-trained models have been successful applied to TOOC tasks, they still struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and real-time inference requirements in counting scenarios. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a real-time TOOC framework, called the Real-Time Counter (RT-Counter), that achieves not only good counting accuracy but also high computational efficiency. RT-Counter designs a novel Visual Prototype Textualization (VPT) module that can project learned visual features into a text feature space and then generate features containing the abstract information that is hard to capture with visual prototypes and the detailed prototype information that is difficult to describe in text, enhancing the object-level visual-language model's counting capabilities. Additionally, RT-Counter incorporates our Weaving Transformer (Weaformer) layers, maintaining high descriptive power at a fraction of the computational cost. The Weaformer layer adopts a novel hybrid attention mechanism that can efficiently weave together local and global visual features. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that RT-Counter successfully breaks the accuracy-speed trade-off in TOOC. While achieving a competitive MAE of 13.30 on FSC147, RT-Counter operates at 112.48 FPS, making it 7.4x faster and over 4$\times$ more parameter-efficient than the existing leading methods in TOOC. Our work aims at balancing high accuracy and real-time performance in TOOC. Code is available at: https://github.com/Jason-Mar1/RT-Counter.

</details>


### [42] [Geometric Consistency Protocol for Foundation Model Features in Multi-View Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17564)
*Qiyan Luo,Jie Yang,Yingdong Pi,Lekang Wen,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向卫星多视图重建的、基于RPC几何约束的标准化评估协议，揭示了语义相似性与几何可匹配性之间的解耦，并证明2D骨干网络在RPC一致评估下与专用3D模型竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的卫星多视图重建评估协议依赖于无约束的2D全局匹配，忽视了RPC模型定义的弯曲、与高度相关的核线几何，导致平面搜索空间在物理上不一致，从而产生误导性基准结果。

Method: 提出一种面向RPC框架的几何忠实且可复现的评估协议，包括：(1) RPC投影的3D一致性度量；(2) 几何约束的稠密匹配代理，评估在物理可行的搜索流形下相似性响应是否保持局部化和唯一性。

Result: 关键发现是语义一致性与几何定位的解耦：投影3D点上的高跨视图相似性并不能保证实际推理中的可靠可匹配性。在RPC一致评估下，最先进的2D骨干网络与专用3D模型相比仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将几何约束纳入评估是卫星影像问题定义的基础，所提协议为遥感基础模型跨传感器和复杂成像几何的迁移提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Standardized evaluation protocols are indispensable for robust benchmarking in remote sensing, particularly as foundation features are increasingly transferred across diverse sensors and complex imaging geometries. In satellite multi-view reconstruction, conventional evaluations relying on unconstrained 2D global matching are often misleading. The Rational Function Model (RFM) and its Rational Polynomial Coefficients (RPC) dictate a curved, height-dependent epipolar geometry that render flat 2D search spaces physically inconsistent. We propose a geometry-faithful and reproducible protocol tailored for the RPC framework. Our approach integrates an RPC-projected 3D consistency metric with a geometry-constrained dense matching proxy, specifically evaluating whether similarity responses remain localized and unique under physically plausible search manifolds. A pivotal finding of our joint reporting strategy is the decoupling of semantic agreement and geometric localization: high cross-view similarity at a projected 3D point does not guarantee reliable matchability in practical inference. Our benchmark demonstrates that incorporating geometric constraints is fundamental to the problem definition in satellite imagery. Furthermore, we show that state-of-the-art 2D backbones remain remarkably competitive against specialized 3D-aware models when subjected to this RPC-consistent evaluation.

</details>


### [43] [Root-Selecting Fixed-Point Inversion for Rectified Flows via Trajectory Straightness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17584)
*Semin Kim,Jihwan Yoon,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SelFix，一种基于固定点求解的逆向方法，通过选择更直的反向轨迹的固定点解来提升图像重建和编辑质量，在FLUX.1-dev和PIE-Bench上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定点逆向方法虽然通过将每个逆向步骤建模为固定点问题来提升精度，但缺乏在多个固定点解中进行选择的原则性机制，而不同的选择会导致重建和编辑质量出现显著差异。

Method: 提出SelFix，一种固定点逆向方法，以轨迹平直度（straightness）作为原则性的选择标准，选择能够诱导更平直逆向轨迹的固定点解，同时在标准局部假设下保持收敛到精确的逆向根。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和PIE-Bench上的实验表明，SelFix改进了固定点逆向方法，在真实图像重建和源保留的提示驱动的图像编辑上均优于现有的逆向基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入轨迹平直度作为固定点解的选择准则，有效解决了固定点逆向中多解选择问题，提升了图像重建与编辑的性能。

Abstract: Finding the initial noise that generates a given data sample, known as inversion, is a key component for downstream applications such as training-free image editing. Existing fixed-point inversion methods improve inversion accuracy by formulating each inversion step as a fixed-point problem, but they lack a principled mechanism for selecting among multiple fixed-point solutions that can arise in practice. We observe that different selections induce different inversion trajectories, leading to substantial variation in reconstruction and editing quality. For rectified flows, we further find that this variation is closely associated with trajectory straightness, motivating straightness as a principled selection criterion. We propose SelFix, a fixed-point inversion method that selects fixed-point solutions inducing straighter inverse trajectories while retaining convergence to an exact inverse root under standard local assumptions. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and PIE-Bench show that SelFix improves fixed-point inversion, achieving stronger real-image reconstruction and better source-preserving prompt-based editing than prior inversion baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/seminkim/selfix.

</details>


### [44] [TivTok: Broadcasting Time-Invariant Tokens for Scalable Video Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17590)
*Weiliang Chen,Yuanhui Huang,Xuebo Wang,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TivTok是一种复用感知的视频分词器，通过时间不变性（TIV）和时间变化性（TV）令牌分解，将持久性信息（如静态背景）在帧间复用，显著提升压缩效率（128帧视频压缩比提升2.91倍），仅需下采样分词器1.1%的令牌量即可达到12.65的rFVD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分词器主要通过压缩视频为更少令牌来提升可扩展性，但反复编码跨帧和块的持久性内容（如静态背景和一致物体外观），导致计算冗余，限制了长视频建模效率。

Method: 提出Scope-Induced Factorization (SIF)，将令牌分为两类：Time-Invariant (TIV)令牌关注整个片段，编码帧间共享信息；Time-Variant (TV)令牌仅关注对应帧及TIV令牌，编码帧特定残差。解码器中使用Invariant Broadcasting (IB)跨帧和块复用同一组TIV令牌，实现并行重建和长视频分词。

Result: 在标准16×256×256基准上达到12.65的rFVD；对128帧视频，压缩效率相比基线提升2.91倍；仅需下采样分词器1.1%的令牌量。

Conclusion: TivTok通过显式分离可复用的持久性信息和帧特定残差，大幅减少冗余令牌，在保持高质量重建的同时显著提升视频分词的可扩展性，为长视频生成提供了高效基础。

Abstract: Video tokenization is fundamental to scalable video generation, as the number of tokens directly determines the computational cost and the length of videos that can be modeled. Existing tokenizers mainly improve scalability by compressing videos into fewer tokens, but they often continue to represent persistent content, such as static backgrounds and consistent object appearances, repeatedly across frames and chunks. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TivTok} (\textit{Time-Invariant Tokenizer}), a reuse-aware video tokenizer that makes persistent information reusable across time. TivTok represents a clip with Time-Invariant (TIV) tokens that encode information shared across frames and Time-Variant (TV) tokens that encode frame-specific residuals. To obtain this factorization, we introduce Scope-Induced Factorization (SIF), which assigns different attention scopes to the two token groups: TIV tokens attend to the full clip, whereas each TV token only accesses its corresponding frame together with the TIV tokens. In the decoder, Invariant Broadcasting (IB) reuses the same TIV tokens across frames and chunks for parallel reconstruction and long-video tokenization. Experiments show that TivTok achieves an rFVD of 12.65 on the standard $16{\times}256{\times}256$ benchmark and improves compression efficiency by 2.91$\times$ for 128-frame videos compared with the evaluated baselines, while using only 1.1\% of the tokens required by downsample-based tokenizers in our evaluation.

</details>


### [45] [Test-Time Training for Robust Text-Guided Open-Vocabulary Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17601)
*Hao-Yuan Ma,Yuda Zou,Li Zhang,Yongchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust-TOOC基准和Dual-TTT框架，解决文本引导开放词汇目标计数在恶劣条件下的鲁棒性问题。Dual-TTT通过在测试时仅训练文本引导轻量去噪模块（TL-Denoiser），保持原始计数网络冻结，有效提升模型在雨、雾、黑暗等六种退化条件下的计数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TOOC方法主要在理想图像上开发和评估，而真实场景常受雨、雾、黑暗、传感器噪声等恶劣条件影响，导致视觉质量严重下降并损害视觉-语言对齐性能。目前缺乏针对TOOC在退化条件下的评估基准和鲁棒性方法。

Method: 提出Dual-TTT双架构测试时训练框架：1) 仅更新Text-guided Lightweight Denoising module (TL-Denoiser)，保持原始计数网络冻结；2) TL-Denoiser受扩散模型启发，优化去除退化条件下的噪声表示；3) 测试时训练无需标注，可无缝集成到现有TOOC模型。

Result: 在多个近期TOOC基线模型上的大量实验证明了该方法的有效性，在六种退化类型（雨、雾、黑暗、高斯噪声、椒盐噪声、混合退化）下均表现良好。

Conclusion: Robust-TOOC是首个评估TOOC在多样退化条件下的基准，Dual-TTT提供了一种无需修改原架构、无需额外标注的鲁棒性提升方案，可广泛适用于现有TOOC方法。

Abstract: Text-guided Open-vocabulary Object Counting (TOOC) enables counting arbitrary object categories specified by text prompts, offering substantially greater flexibility than conventional closed-set counting. However, existing TOOC methods are developed and evaluated primarily on ideal images, while real-world scenes often suffer from adverse conditions such as rain, fog, darkness, and sensor noise, which severely degrade visual quality and impair vision-language alignment. To bridge this gap, we introduce Robust-TOOC, the first benchmark for evaluating TOOC under diverse corruption conditions, which covers six representative degradation types: rain, fog, darkness, Gaussian noise, salt-and-pepper noise, and mixed corruption. To improve robustness while preserving the original counting architecture, we propose Dual-TTT, a dual-architecture test-time training framework for TOOC. Specifically, during test-time training, Dual-TTT updates only the Text-guided Lightweight Denoising module (TL-Denoiser), while keeping the original counting network frozen. Inspired by diffusion models, the TL-Denoiser is optimized to remove corruption-aware noise from image representations under degraded conditions. Since only the TL-Denoiser is trained at test time, Dual-TTT is annotation-free and can be seamlessly integrated into existing TOOC models without modifying their original architecture. Extensive experiments on multiple recent TOOC baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [46] [Flux-Guard: Facial Identity Protection using diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17606)
*Jie Wang,Tao Wang,Ru Zhang,Jianyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux-Guard，一种基于对抗攻击的隐私保护人脸编辑框架，在统一生成过程中同时实现人脸编辑和隐私保护，通过流轨迹控制方法和潜在空间对抗优化，有效防止编辑后的人脸图像被恶意人脸识别系统追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗隐私保护方法虽然能降低未授权人脸识别性能，但与生成式人脸编辑不兼容；而当前编辑方法往往保留身份特征，使编辑后图像仍可被恶意人脸识别系统追踪。用户对个性化肖像生成和社交分享的需求日益增长，亟需一种既能编辑人脸又能保护隐私的解决方案。

Method: 1) 设计流轨迹控制方法，将语义操作与生成过程对齐；2) 引入潜在空间对抗优化，结合自适应感知损失驱动的加权策略，动态调整对抗强度，在保持视觉质量的同时最大化攻击效果。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ和LADN数据集上，Flux-Guard支持人脸编辑并显著提升对跨域人脸识别模型的攻击成功率；商业API评估也证实了其在真实应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: Flux-Guard成功将人脸编辑与隐私保护集成于统一框架，实现了编辑功能与对抗攻击的平衡，为社交分享场景下的隐私保护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of face recognition (FR) systems exposes personal images shared on social media and public platforms to identity linkage and privacy risks. Existing adversarial privacy protection methods can degrade unauthorized FR performance but are not compatible with generative face editing. Artificial intelligence-driven face editing tools are gaining popularity, which has significantly increased user demand for personalized portrait generation and social sharing. However, current editing methods often preserve identity features, making the edited images still susceptible to tracking by malicious FR systems. Thus, this paper proposes Flux-Guard, a privacy-preserving face editing framework based on adversarial attacks, which integrates face editing and privacy protection within a unified generative process. Specifically, we design a flow trajectory control method to align semantic manipulations with the generative process and introduce latent-space adversarial optimization with an adaptive perceptual-loss-driven weighting strategy, dynamically adjusting adversarial strength to maximize attack effectiveness while preserving visual quality.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that Flux-Guard supports face editing while significantly improving attack success rates against cross-domain face recognition models on the CelebA-HQ and LADN datasets. Furthermore, evaluation results for commercial APIs have confirmed its effectiveness in real-world applications. The code is released at https://github.com/JLMWang/Flux-Guard.

</details>


### [47] [SkillMoV: Mixture-of-View Routing with Prototype-Conditioned Gating for Unified Multi-View Proficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17615)
*Edoardo Bianchi,Antonio Liotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkillMoV是一个统一的、参数高效的框架，用于从同步多视角视频进行多场景技能水平估计。其核心是Mixture-of-View Projector (MoVP)，通过混合专家范式适配相机特定视角特征，在EgoExo4D数据集六个技能领域上，Exos设置下达到50.17%的总体准确率，超越最强基线3.57个百分点，且仅训练23.32%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常聚焦于单一场景或依赖共享的多视角聚合，难以适应异构相机视角和活动领域。作者希望提出一个统一的、参数高效的框架，能够处理多种场景下的技能评估问题，并利用多视角视频中的互补信息。

Method: SkillMoV框架核心是Mixture-of-View Projector (MoVP)，包含四个阶段：(i) Mixture-of-View软路由器，包含12个专家MLP，无需相机身份监督即可学习视角相关专家偏好；(ii) 跨视角注意力机制对齐同步相机；(iii) 可学习原型锚定，以类别级参考向量条件化表示；(iv) 原型条件化门控投影，生成最终技能嵌入。整体通过LoRA进行参数高效微调。

Result: 在EgoExo4D六个技能领域上，Exos设置下总体准确率50.17%，超越最强Exos基线3.57个百分点；Ego+Exos设置下47.63% vs 48.20%。消融实验：MoV路由贡献+6.61pp，跨视角注意力+4.92pp，原型锚定+4.07pp，随机视角丢弃+3.90pp。仅训练23.32%的参数。

Conclusion: SkillMoV证明了通过混合专家范式适配多视角特征、结合跨视角对齐与原型条件化，可以在统一的框架下有效提升多场景技能评估性能，同时保持参数高效性。该方法在多数场景下超越了现有方法，为多视角视频技能评估提供了新的统一范式。

Abstract: Estimating human proficiency from video is a key challenge for automated skill assessment, with applications in sports coaching, music pedagogy, surgical training, and workplace learning. Existing approaches often focus on individual scenarios or rely on shared multi-view aggregation, limiting their ability to adapt to heterogeneous camera viewpoints and activity domains. We introduce SkillMoV, a unified, parameter-efficient framework for multi-scenario proficiency estimation from synchronized multi-view video. At its core, SkillMoV introduces a Mixture-of-View Projector (MoVP), which adapts the mixture-of-experts paradigm to camera-specific view features. MoVP is composed of four stages: (i) a Mixture-of-View soft router with twelve expert MLPs that learns view-dependent expert preferences without camera-identity supervision; (ii) cross-view attention to align synchronized cameras; (iii) learnable prototype anchoring to condition the representation on class-level reference vectors; and (iv) a prototype-conditioned gated projection that produces the final skill embedding. We evaluate SkillMoV on EgoExo4D across six skill domains and three separately trained view configurations: Ego, Exos, and Ego+Exos. SkillMoV reaches 50.17% overall accuracy in the Exos setting with a single model trained jointly across all scenarios, surpassing the strongest reported Exos result among the compared methods by 3.57 percentage points. In Ego+Exos, SkillMoV remains close to the best reported result in that setting (47.63% versus 48.20%). Ablations on the selected Exos configuration validate each component: MoV routing contributes +6.61 pp over attentive aggregation, cross-view attention +4.92 pp, prototype anchoring +4.07 pp, and stochastic view dropout +3.90 pp. Through LoRA adaptation, SkillMoV trains only 23.32% of its parameters and adds limited measured overhead relative to a LoRA-only baseline.

</details>


### [48] [RAVA: Retrieval-Augmented Viewpoint Alignment for Subject-Driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17619)
*Qiwei Yan,Zhiqiang Yuan,Chongyang Li,Jiapei Zhang,Ying Deng,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVA框架，通过检索增强的几何先验实现跨主体视角对齐，解决了多参考图像生成中因语义相关性导致的视角漂移和结构错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考驱动图像生成在身份保持方面进展迅速，但跨主体的可靠视角控制仍不完善。模型需仅凭图像级证据推断一个主体的隐含视角并迁移到另一主体，现有生成器常依赖虚假语义相关导致视角漂移、部件级结构错配及目标特定内容缺失。

Method: RAVA包括三阶段：首先学习跨实例视角嵌入以检索与锚定视角对齐的目标主体图像；然后应用基于LogDet的子集选择策略保留视角一致且结构互补的紧凑参考集；最后将所选参考输入微调的多参考图像生成器。

Result: 通用语义嵌入在该任务上几乎随机，而提出的检索器显著提升了视角检索质量。在跨主体生成上，RAVA在相同生成骨干下持续优于零样本基线和更强的检索替代方案。

Conclusion: 跨主体视角对齐受益于检索增强的几何基础，而非仅依赖端到端生成。

Abstract: Reference-driven image generation has made rapid progress on identity preservation, but reliable viewpoint control across different subjects remains poorly understood. The difficulty is not merely generating a new image of the target subject: the model must infer the implicit viewpoint of one subject and transfer it to another subject using only image-level evidence, without camera poses, depth, or ray-based conditions. In this setting, existing generators conditioned on multiple image references often rely on spurious semantic correlations, which lead to viewpoint drift, part-level structural mismatches, and missing or unsupported target-specific content. We formulate this challenge as cross-subject viewpoint alignment and propose RAVA, a retrieval-augmented framework that supplies explicit geometric evidence before generation. RAVA first learns a cross-instance viewpoint embedding that retrieves target-subject images aligned with the anchor viewpoint, then applies a LogDet-based subset selection strategy to retain a compact reference set that is both view-consistent and structurally complementary. The selected references are finally consumed by a fine-tuned multi-reference image generator. Experiments show that generic semantic embeddings are nearly random for this task, while the proposed retriever substantially improves viewpoint retrieval quality. On cross-subject generation, RAVA consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines and stronger retrieval alternatives under the same generation backbone. These results indicate that cross-subject viewpoint alignment benefits from retrieval-augmented geometric grounding rather than relying on end-to-end generation alone.

</details>


### [49] [Divide, Deliberate, Decide: A Multi-Agent Framework for Fine-Grained Egocentric Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17627)
*Alessandro Sottovia,Alessandro Torcinovich,Oswald Lanz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Divide, Deliberate, Decide框架，通过异构VLM专家协作与结构化审议，在无需微调的情况下提升零样本细粒度动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度第一人称视频动作识别中，视觉语言模型容易偏向于小部分视觉线索，单一模型难以准确区分动作间的细微差异。

Method: (i) VLM编排器分割视频并生成每段Top-K候选标签；(ii) 来自不同开源模型家族的异构VLM专家进行结构化审议，包括同行咨询环节；(iii) 使用Borda计数聚合专家排名，编排器根据专家证据重新排序自身预测。

Result: 该方法在零样本动作识别性能上优于基线，性能提升源于解耦的模型先验而非额外计算。

Conclusion: 异构审议步骤对性能提升具有显著影响，框架完全本地运行且无需微调。

Abstract: Fine-grained action recognition in egocentric video is challenging for Vision-Language Models (VLMs): actions often differ only in small visual cues, and a single model tends to be biased toward a subset of these cues. We propose Divide, Deliberate, Decide, a fully-local, zero-shot multi-agent framework in which (i) a VLM orchestrator chunks the video and proposes a top-k candidate label list per segment, (ii) an ensemble of heterogeneous VLM specialists, drawn from different open model families, engages in a structured deliberation that includes a peer-consultation round of questions, and (iii) agent rankings are aggregated with a Borda count and the orchestrator re-ranks its own prediction in light of the specialists' evidence. The entire pipeline runs locally with no fine-tuning. Experiments show that our method positively improves zero-shot action recognition performance over the baseline, highlighting the influence of a heterogeneous deliberation step, showing that the gain stems from decorrelated model priors rather than from additional compute.

</details>


### [50] [Bounding Box Label Propagation for Re-Annotation of Document Layout Analysis Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17644)
*Nick Jochum,Tobias Alt-Veit,Christian Schön,Alexander Lück,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BBLP（边界框标签传播）框架，利用视觉、文本和位置嵌入进行标签传播，在仅使用10%标注数据的情况下，在D4LA版面分析数据集上达到54.0% mAP（全监督性能的81.6%），有效减少了目标检测中的重标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际文档处理场景中，数据集随时间增长且类别标注持续优化，导致大量繁重且昂贵的重标注工作。现有方法仅适用于分类任务，缺乏针对目标检测实例重分类（如文档版面分析）的解决方案。

Method: 提出BBLP伪标签框架：1) 构建目标编码器，融合视觉、文本和位置嵌入得到联合嵌入表示；2) 基于该嵌入在部分标注数据集上进行标签传播；3) 采用即插即用方式集成到现有目标检测流程中。

Result: 在D4LA版面分析数据集上，仅使用10%标注数据即达到54.0% mAP，相当于全监督性能的81.6%。

Conclusion: BBLP证明了标签传播在目标检测中的潜力，为减少真实文档处理应用中的手动标注工作量奠定了基础。

Abstract: Datasets in practical document processing scenarios typically grow over time, and their class annotations undergo continuous refinement. This creates significant re-annotation efforts, which are time-consuming and costly. A promising remedy is to re-annotate only a small subset of available documents manually and apply semi-supervised learning techniques that leverage both labelled and unlabelled data. Although there are numerous approaches to tackle this problem for classification, there exists no adaptation for the problem of re-classifying object detection instances, e.g. for document layout analysis. To this end, we propose Bounding Box Label Propagation (BBLP), a pseudo-labelling framework for object detection. An object encoder integrates visual, textual, and positional embeddings from object detection samples to come up with a joint embedding that can be used for Label Propagation on partially annotated datasets in a plug-and-play fashion. Evaluation results indicate that the proposed approach produces high-quality class annotations of bounding boxes. In the D4LA layout analysis dataset, it achieves a mAP of 54.0%, corresponding to 81.6% of fully supervised performance, while using only 10% labelled data. Our work demonstrates the potential of Label Propagation for object detection and lays the groundwork for reducing manual annotation efforts in real-world document processing applications.

</details>


### [51] [Adaptive Volumetric Mechanical Property Fields Invariant to Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18231)
*Rishit Dagli,Donglai Xiang,Vismay Modi,Xuning Yang,Gavriel State,David I. W. Levin,Maria Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaVoMP提出了一种基于稀疏自适应体素结构(SAV)的Transformer编码器-解码器模型，能够为任意3D物体预测高分辨率（相比先前方法提升16³倍）的空间变化材料属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度），在提高精度和内存效率的同时降低了测试时计算量，从而支持复杂3D物体直接转换为可进行真实物理仿真的资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数3D资产缺乏准确的物理材料属性（杨氏模量、泊松比、密度），而这些属性对于数字世界的可靠物理仿真至关重要。现有方法在分辨率、精度和内存效率方面存在不足，亟需一种能够为任意3D物体预测高分辨率空间变化材料属性的方法。

Method: 提出稀疏自适应体素结构(SAV)高效表示输入3D形状和材料场；用稀疏Transformer编码器-解码器模型替代先前方法VoMP的固定体素模型，通过自回归方式为每个输入形状生成独特的SAV来表示其材料属性，实现了相比先前方法16³倍的分辨率提升。

Result: AdaVoMP在估计体积属性方面比所有先前方法更准确，同时测试时计算量更低；能够将高分辨率复杂3D物体转换为可直接用于物理仿真的资产，产生逼真的可变形仿真结果。

Conclusion: AdaVoMP通过稀疏自适应体素结构和Transformer模型有效解决了3D物体材料属性预测中的分辨率、精度和效率问题，为数字世界的物理仿真提供了更优质的资产生成方案。

Abstract: Accurate mechanical properties (or materials) Young's modulus ($E$), Poisson's ratio ($ν$) and density ($ρ$) are essential for reliable physics simulation of digital worlds, but most 3D assets lack this information. We propose AdaVoMP, a method for predicting accurate dense spatially-varying ($E$, $ν$, $ρ$) for input 3D objects across representations, improving the resolution, accuracy, and memory efficiency over the state-of-the-art. The foundation of our technique is a sparse and adaptive voxel structure SAV that efficiently represents both the input 3D shape and the material field output. We replace the fixed-voxel model of the most accurate prior method, VoMP, with a novel sparse transformer encoder-decoder model that learns to generate a unique SAV autoregressively for every input shape to represent its materials, achieving a resolution $16^3\times$ higher than prior art. Experiments show that AdaVoMP estimates more accurate volumetric properties, even with lesser test-time compute than all prior art. This allows us to convert high-resolution complex 3D objects into simulation-ready assets, resulting in realistic deformable simulations.

</details>


### [52] [MambaCount: Efficient Text-guided Open-vocabulary Object Counting with Spatial Sparse State Space Duality Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17650)
*Hao-Yuan Ma,Li Zhang,Minjie Qiang,Jie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MambaCount提出基于空间稀疏状态空间对偶(S⁴D)块的高效框架，通过重构Mamba隐藏状态衰减动力学解决因果建模的空间依赖限制，并引入空间令牌选择子块降低高熵问题，配合多粒度原型实现跨模态对齐，在FSC-147上以线性复杂度取得SOTA性能(MAE=12.23)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TOOC方法依赖Transformer，其二次复杂度限制了高分辨率场景的扩展性；Mamba虽有线性复杂度优势，但因果公式限制了非因果视觉任务所需的空间双向依赖建模，且现有Mamba视觉模型忽略了空间令牌响应中不受控的高熵问题，这会削弱局部细节和高频线索。

Method: 1) 分析并重构Mamba隐藏状态的衰减动力学以缓解因果建模带来的依赖约束；2) 引入空间令牌选择(STS)子块降低空间令牌响应中的高熵；3) 设计多粒度原型(MGP)在不同语义级别识别类物区域，改善跨模态对齐与可解释性。

Result: 在FSC-147数据集上，MambaCount在无需二次查询的方法中取得SOTA性能，测试MAE为12.23，同时保持线性复杂度。

Conclusion: MambaCount通过S⁴D块有效解决了Mamba在视觉任务中的因果依赖和高熵问题，实现了线性复杂度下的高精度开放词汇目标计数，验证了Mamba在密集场景计数任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Text-guided Open-vocabulary Object Counting (TOOC) aims to estimate the number of objects described by text prompts, which is particularly challenging in dense scenes with large scale variations. Existing TOOC approaches predominantly rely on Transformers, whose quadratic complexity with respect to image resolution limits their scalability. Mamba offers a promising alternative due to its linear complexity. However, previous Mamba-based methods have two main limitations. On the one hand, the inherent causal formulation of Mamba constrains the bidirectional spatial dependency modeling required by non-causal vision tasks. On the other hand, existing Mamba-based vision models often overlook the unconstrained high entropy in the spatial token responses, which can weaken local details and high-frequency cues. To address these limitations, we propose MambaCount, an efficient framework built on the Spatial Sparse State Space Duality (S^4D) block. Specifically, we analyze and reconstruct the decay dynamics of hidden states in Mamba to alleviate the dependency constraints introduced by causal modeling. Moreover, we introduce a Spatial Token Selection (STS) sub-block to reduce the unconstrained high entropy in spatial token responses within Mamba. In addition, we design Multi-Granularity Prototypes (MGP) to identify object-like regions at different semantic levels, improving cross-modal alignment and interpretability. Extensive experiments on FSC-147 demonstrate that MambaCount achieves state-of-the-art performance among methods without secondary querying, obtaining a test MAE of 12.23, while retaining linear complexity.

</details>


### [53] [MOCHI: Motion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18243)
*Jiye Lee,Yonghun Choi,Jungdam Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOCHI框架，通过两阶段优化（物理合理手部抓取生成+扩散噪声优化）增强多人-物体交互数据质量，解决接触错位、运动抖动和手指细节缺失等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人-物体交互场景的复杂性导致现有捕捉数据存在接触错位、运动抖动、手指细节缺失等噪声问题，亟需高质量数据增强方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）从含噪声人体输入优化生成物理合理且语义一致的抓取姿态，并扩展为完整手-物交互序列；2）基于扩散模型的噪声优化框架，利用单人运动先验并引入人-物和人-人交互约束来优化全身运动。

Result: 在多种MHOI数据（捕捉数据或生成模型合成数据）上验证了有效性，展示了在不同参与人数和交互类型下的鲁棒性，并支持关键帧创建和几何数据增强等应用。

Conclusion: MOCHI能有效增强多人-物体交互数据质量，具备良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Collaborative human-object interaction shows dynamic and complex movements that require mutual anticipation and continuous adjustment between participants and the shared object. Modeling such collaborative multi-human object interaction (MHOI) scenarios requires high-quality data acquisition as a foundational step; however, this is challenging due to the inherent complexity of MHOI where human-human and human-object interactions occur simultaneously. Such complexity leads to noisy MHOI captures characterized by several artifacts: contact misalignment between hands and objects, motion jitter and temporal inconsistencies in the captured sequences, and missing or incomplete finger-level articulation details. To address these challenges, we present MOCHI (MOtion Enhancement of Collaborative Human-object Interactions), a two-stage framework for enhancing noisy MHOI data. Our approach first generates physically plausible hand grasps through optimization from noisy body input, producing grasps that are both physically plausible and semantically consistent with the body pose, where these optimized grasps are extended into complete hand-object interaction sequences. Consequently, the full-body motion for all participants are refined through a diffusion-based noise optimization framework that uses single-person motion priors. During the optimization process, we introduce optimization objectives to encode human-object and human-human interaction information within these single-person priors. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our pipeline across diverse MHOI data, either acquired by existing capture methods or synthesized by generative models. We further show robustness of our system across varying numbers of participants and types of interactions, and demonstrate various applications including keyframe-based MHOI creation and data augmentation through varying object geometries.

</details>


### [54] [Do We Really Need Diffusion? A Fast U-Net for Paired Medical Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17675)
*Alicia Pirwass,Birte Glimm,Michael Munz,Hans-Joachim Wilke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨能否从常规T2加权MRI中通过图像到图像翻译估算脂肪分数（MRI-SFF）。在23万张图像的大规模数据集上，轻量级U-Net在准确性和速度上均优于扩散概率模型（DDPM），相关系数达0.975，MAE为0.014，推理速度比DDPM快208倍，支持实时临床应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI-SFF是代谢和肌肉骨骼疾病的重要生物标志物，但其获取需要专门的MRI序列，在常规临床中不可用。作者希望从广泛可用的T2加权MRI中估算SFF，从而降低临床门槛。

Method: 使用230048张配对2D图像（来自德国国家队列NAKO），比较4层U-Net与去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的图像到图像翻译性能，以身份映射作为基线。

Result: 轻量级U-Net在Pearson相关系数（r=0.975 vs 0.962）和MAE（0.014±0.015 vs 0.019±0.019）上均优于DDPM，且推理时间仅为25.2ms（DDPM为5227.2ms，采用50步DDIM），速度提升208倍。

Conclusion: 轻量级U-Net能以极低计算代价实现临床级SFF估计性能，有望实现实时临床部署。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging-signal fat fraction (MRI-SFF) quantifies tissue fat and serves as an established biomarker for metabolic and musculoskeletal disorders. The acquisition requires, however, specialized MRI sequences, which are not available routinely. We investigate whether SFF can be estimated from widely available T2-weighted (T2w) MRI via image-to-image translation (I2I). We further compare a lightweight 4-level U-Net to a state-of-the-art Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) using a dataset of 230 048 paired 2D images (183 517 train, 23 621 val, 22 910 test) from the German National Cohort (NAKO). Both models clearly outperform the identity baseline (Pearson correlation r = 0.769, mean absolute error MAE = 0.070 +/- 0.054), which confirms that the models learn a non-trivial cross-modal mapping. Interestingly, the lightweight U-Net outperforms the DDPM in both correlation (r = 0.975 vs. 0.962) and error (MAE = 0.014 +/- 0.015 vs. 0.019 +/- 0.019), while reducing inference time by a factor of 208 (25.2 ms vs. 5 227.2 ms per image using 50 Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) steps). The strong clinical performance at substantially reduced computational cost enables real-time clinical use.

</details>


### [55] [See First, Answer Later: Visual Evidence Pre-Alignment via Sufficiency-Driven RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17678)
*Yilian Liu,Sicong Leng,Guoshun Nan,Junyi Zhu,Jiayu Huang,Minghao Sun,Xuancheng Zhu,Yisong Chen,Zexian Wei,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉证据预对齐（VEPA），在预训练和后训练之间引入中间阶段，通过GRPO优化以问题为条件的视觉证据描述，增强MLLMs对细粒度视觉信息的利用，从而提升视觉密集型任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs虽融合文本推理与视觉输入，但存在响应与图像不一致的问题，表明推理过程中未有效利用视觉证据。传统预训练范式仅提供弱视觉对齐：短而粗略的标题使模型偏向显著物体而忽略细粒度视觉证据。

Method: 提出视觉证据预对齐（VEPA），在预训练和后训练之间引入中间阶段，采用基于充分性的目标函数结合组相对策略优化（GRPO）来优化以问题为条件的视觉证据描述。

Result: 跨多个基准的大量实验表明，VEPA持续提升了视觉要求较高的评估任务上的性能，并能与标准监督后训练互补。进一步分析表明，性能提升源于增强的、可迁移的视觉基础能力，而非额外的任务特定训练。

Conclusion: VEPA通过中间阶段的视觉证据预对齐，有效弥补了现有预训练范式中视觉基础薄弱的缺陷，为提升MLLMs视觉推理能力提供了一种有效且可迁移的训练策略。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) integrate strong text reasoning with visual inputs, yet their responses can be inconsistent with the underlying images, indicating ineffective utilization of visual evidence during inference. The prevailing training paradigm relies on large-scale caption-based pretraining for general alignment, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning to enable instruction following and complex reasoning. However, such pretraining provides only weak visual grounding: short, coarse captions bias models toward salient objects while neglecting fine-grained visual evidence. In this paper, we introduce Visual Evidence Pre-Alignment (VEPA), an intermediate stage between pretraining and post-training that explores a novel sufficiency-driven objective with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to optimize question-conditioned visual evidence descriptions. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that our VEPA consistently enhances performance on visually demanding evaluations and complements standard supervised post-training. Further analyses show that the income stems from strengthened, transferable visual grounding, rather than from additional task-specific training.

</details>


### [56] [SegTME-UNI2: A Foundation Model-Based Framework for Generalisable Multiclass Cell Segmentation and LLM-Driven Tumour Microenvironment Characterisation in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17702)
*Wan Siti Halimatul Munirah Wan Ahmad,Faris Syahmi Samidi,Mohammad Badal Ahmmed,Vimal Angela Thiviyanathan,Selvam James Thavaraj,Anwar P. P. Abdul Majeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SEGTME-UNI2框架，核心是UNI2-UPERHOVER双头分割模型（基于UNI2-H基础模型+双UperNet解码器），通过三阶段渐进式伪标签课程（PanNuke→TCGA-UT单尺度→多尺度）实现细胞分割，并提取20+种TME特征，最后用微调NVIDIA BioNeMo GPT生成临床可解释的TME叙述报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从常规H&E染色组织学图像中表征肿瘤微环境（TME）需要同时实现细胞分割、特征提取和可解释的临床报告，现有方法难以兼顾这三方面需求。

Method: 提出SEGTME-UNI2框架：（1）核心为UNI2-UPERHOVER双头分割模型，基于UNI2-H病理基础模型（ViT-Giant，在超1亿张图块上预训练），搭配两个并行UperNet解码器——一个用于六类语义分割，一个用于水平-垂直梯度回归实现基于分水岭的细胞核实例分离；（2）三阶段渐进式伪标签课程（Stage1: PanNuke人工标注→Stage2: TCGA-UT单尺度熵过滤伪标签→Stage3: 六分辨率尺度全部图块）；（3）TME特征提取管线计算20+种组成、形态、空间熵和细胞间距离指标；（4）特征经JSON编码输入微调NVIDIA BioNeMo GPT生成临床可解释TME叙述。

Result: 在保留的PanNuke和TCGA-UT测试集上初步验证了框架的可行性和内部一致性；公开了伪标签TCGA-UT数据集和UNI2-UPERHOVER模型检查点。

Conclusion: SEGTME-UNI2能够从H&E图像中实现统一的细胞分割、TME特征提取和临床报告生成，伪标签课程有效缓解了像素级标注不足的问题，为大规模TME分析和空间生物学研究提供了基础工具。

Abstract: Characterising the tumour microenvironment (TME) from routine H&E-stained histology images requires simultaneous cell segmentation, feature extraction, and interpretable clinical reporting. We present SEGTME-UNI2, a unified framework addressing these requirements. Its core is UNI2-UPERHOVER, a dual-head segmentation model pairing the UNI2-H pathology foundation model (ViT-Giant, pretrained on >100M tiles from 100K slides) with two parallel UperNet decoders: one for six-class semantic segmentation and one for horizontal-vertical gradient regression enabling watershed-based nuclear instance separation. To address the lack of pixel-level annotations in large real-world repositories, UNI2-UPERHOVER undergoes a three-stage progressive pseudo-label curriculum. Each stage trains a fresh model without weight transfer, driving improvement entirely via increased pseudo-label quality: Stage 1: Uses human-annotated PanNuke (7,901 images, 189,744 nuclei, 0.25 um/pixel). Stage 2: Uses entropy-filtered pseudo-labels from the Stage 1 model on 271,711 TCGA-UT scale-0 patches (0.5 um/pixel). Stage 3: Uses pseudo-labels from the Stage 2 model on all 1,608,060 TCGA-UT patches across six resolution scales (0.5-1.0 um/pixel). Segmentation outputs feed a structured TME feature extraction pipeline computing 20+ per-patch compositional, morphological, spatial entropy, and intercellular distance metrics. These are encoded as JSON and passed to a fine-tuned NVIDIA BioNeMo GPT model to generate clinically interpretable TME narratives. Preliminary validation on held-out PanNuke and TCGA-UT partitions demonstrates framework feasibility and internal consistency. The pseudo-labelled TCGA-UT dataset and UNI2-UPERHOVER checkpoint are publicly released to support large-scale TME profiling and spatial biology research.

</details>


### [57] [Vision-language models for chest radiography do not always need the image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17710)
*Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Lisa Adams,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有医学视觉语言模型的高准确率可能源于利用文本先验而非真正理解图像，本文提出因果审计方法区分模型是否真正使用图像，发现部分大模型表现与纯文本模型无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉-语言模型在胸部X光片报告生成任务中表现优异，但这可能源于模型利用文本中的疾病名称先验信息，而非真正理解图像内容。标准基准无法区分这两种情况，导致模型性能评估存在严重偏差，阻碍了可信的临床部署。

Method: 提出因果审计方法：对图像进行三种干预操作（遮挡相关区域、遮挡无关区域、替换为同一标签的其他患者图像），并结合三种行为指标测试模型的正确答案是否真正依赖于图像内容。

Result: 九种系统中，纯文本模型（无图像输入）与最佳多模态模型仅相差5.7个准确率百分点；一个1190亿参数的多模态模型与70亿参数的纯文本基线模型在统计上无法区分。审计将系统分为三类：忽略图像的模型（3个）、不稳定的模型（1个）、选择性使用图像的模型（5个）。纯文本模型与放射科医生的准确率在统计上相当，但接地率为零；使用图像的模型接地率与放射科医生相当。

Conclusion: 准确率不能作为临床部署的可靠指标，接地审计（而非准确率）应成为临床准入标准。报告置信度仅在使用图像时才有助于识别不可靠回答。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models report strong chest radiograph accuracy, and this is increasingly read as evidence that they use the image. That inference is unsafe: a model exploiting finding-name priors scores like one that reads the scan, and no standard benchmark separates them. We introduce a causal audit that intervenes on the image, occluding the relevant region, occluding an irrelevant one, and swapping in another patient's same-label scan, and combines three behavioral metrics to test whether a correct answer depends on the image. Across nine systems, a text-only model with no image access reaches within 5.7 accuracy points of the best multimodal one, and a 119-billion-parameter multimodal model is statistically indistinguishable from a 7-billion text-only baseline. The audit splits the cohort into three models that ignore the image, one that is unstable, and five that use it selectively, for a subset of findings; the categories hold across a second dataset, resolution, and prompt phrasing. Against board-certified radiologists, a text-only model is statistically indistinguishable from a radiologist's accuracy while grounding at zero, whereas the image-using models ground at radiologist-comparable rates. Reported confidence flags ungrounded answers only when a model uses the image. Grounding audits, not accuracy, should gate clinical deployment.

</details>


### [58] [Structured Adversarial Camouflage via Voronoi Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17711)
*Jens Bayer,Stefan Becker,David Münch,Michael Arens,Jürgen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Voronoi图的对抗性伪装攻击方法，仅优化种子点位置并在固定可打印调色板下通过软分配生成结构化伪装图案，有效降低行人检测性能，具有参数高效、视觉隐蔽性好的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逐像素对抗补丁计算开销大且视觉上易被察觉，限制了在安全关键系统中的应用，亟需更高效、视觉更隐蔽的对抗攻击方法。

Method: 采用Voronoi图结构，在固定可打印调色板约束下仅优化种子点位置，通过软分配机制生成类迷彩的拼接图案，无需额外正则化；在服装级（通过分割掩码应用）和朴素放置两种方式下进行攻击。

Result: 朴素放置（Inria→COCO）效果较差，而基于分割掩码的服装级应用（3DPeople）导致AP显著下降；攻击可迁移至域外背景及多种YOLO检测器（v9/10/11/12）；更换调色板可基本抵消攻击效果，单色调整容忍度有限（≤0.17）。

Conclusion: 参数高效、受调色板约束的Voronoi伪装设计在提升视觉合理性的同时有效降低了实时检测器性能，且攻击具有黑盒鲁棒性；攻击效果依赖于结构-调色板的耦合关系，物理验证和颜色校准留待未来工作。

Abstract: Pixel-wise adversarial patches are computationally heavy and often visually detectable, limiting utility in security-critical systems. We present adversarial Voronoi camouflage that optimizes only seed-point locations under fixed, printable palettes using a soft assignment, producing structured, splinter camouflage-like patterns without additional regularization. Evaluated on person detection with COCO-style AP@[.5:.95], naive placement (Inria -> COCO) performs comparably bad, while garment-level application via segmentation mask (3DPeople) results in a significant AP drop. The attack transfers to out-of-domain backgrounds and across detector families (YOLOv9/10/11/12), indicating robustness in black-box settings. Repainting with different palettes largely nullifies the effect, and single-color tweaks show limited tolerance (<=0.17), highlighting a structure-palette coupling. The parameter-efficient, palette-constrained design improves visual plausibility while degrading real-time detector performance. Physical validation and color calibration are left for future work.
  Code: https://github.com/JensBayer/Voronoi
  This paper was originally presented at the International Conference on Military Communication and Information Systems (ICMCIS), organized by the Information Systems Technology (IST) Scientific and Technical Committee, IST-224-RSY - the ICMCIS, held in Bath, United Kingdom, 12-13 May 2026.

</details>


### [59] [Heterogeneous SAR-optical fusion for near-real-time land use and land cover mapping under cloud contamination: A novel framework and global benchmark dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17713)
*Jiangong Xu,Weibao Xue,Xiaoyu Yu,Jun Pan,Xinlian Lianga,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudLULC-Net，一种端到端异源SAR-光学融合框架，用于从云污染Sentinel-2影像和时序邻近Sentinel-1 SAR数据直接预测LULC图，并构建了含40,223组三元组的大规模基准数据集CloudLULC-Set。实验表明该方法在云污染场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学遥感影像常受云和云阴影污染，限制了其在近实时土地利用/覆盖制图中的可靠性；现有SAR-光学融合方法通常假设光学观测可靠，未能充分处理云污染引入的语义不确定性。

Method: 提出CloudLULC-Net，包含三个核心模块：(1) 光学可靠性调制——抑制不可靠的光学响应；(2) 异源信息自适应聚合——建模光学与SAR表示间的高阶空间-通道交互；(3) 统一语义映射Transformer——将融合特征组织到LULC导向的潜空间。此外引入语义锚点引导优化策略以提升中间语义表示的一致性。同时构建了CloudLULC-Set基准数据集。

Result: CloudLULC-Net在测试集上达到OA 86.60%、F1-score 83.29%、mIoU 73.51%，优于代表性异源重建优先方法和端到端SAR-光学映射方法。与现有全球LULC产品比较及不同云覆盖水平下的分析进一步验证了其鲁棒性和实用价值。

Conclusion: CloudLULC-Net有效解决了云污染场景下光学- SAR融合制图的语义不确定性问题，为多云区域近实时LULC制图提供了鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Optical remote sensing imagery is frequently degraded by cloud and cloud-shadow contamination, which limits its reliability for near-real-time land use and land cover (LULC) mapping. Although synthetic aperture radar (SAR) can provide cloud-penetrating structural information, existing SAR-optical fusion methods often assume reliable optical observations and insufficiently address the semantic uncertainty introduced by cloud contamination. To address this issue, we propose CloudLULC-Net, an end-to-end heterogeneous SAR-optical fusion framework that directly predicts LULC maps from cloud-contaminated Sentinel-2 imagery and temporally adjacent Sentinel-1 SAR observations. The proposed network incorporates optical reliability modulation to suppress unreliable optical responses, heterogeneous information adaptive aggregation to model high-order spatial-channel interactions between optical and SAR representations, and a unified semantic mapping transformer to organize fused features in a LULC-oriented latent space. A semantic anchor-guided optimization strategy is further introduced to improve the consistency of intermediate semantic representations. To support this task, we construct CloudLULC-Set, a large-scale benchmark dataset containing 40,223 curated SAR-optical-label triplets with pixel-level LULC annotations across diverse geographic regions and cloud conditions. Experimental results show that CloudLULC-Net achieves an OA of 86.60%, an F1-score of 83.29%, and an mIoU of 73.51%, outperforming representative heterogeneous reconstruction-first and end-to-end SAR-optical mapping methods. Comparisons with existing global LULC products and analyses under different cloud-cover levels further demonstrate the robustness and practical value of CloudLULC-Net for target-date LULC mapping in cloud-prone regions.The project is publicly available at: https://github.com/RSIIPAC/CloudLULC

</details>


### [60] [GSPan: A Continuous Gaussian Primitive Representation for Arbitrary-Scale Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17722)
*Fangyi Li,Xiaoyuan Yang,Yixiao Li,Zongyang Sui,Kangqing Shen,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSPan将2D高斯溅射引入全色锐化，通过连续可学习的2D高斯原语表示波段级残差细节，替代传统固定网格预测。结合双流层次交互架构和空间-光谱交互注意力模块，实现任意尺度融合和高效的尺度解耦不对称推理，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将全色锐化视为固定网格预测，限制了尺度自适应能力，无法灵活适应不同分辨率的融合需求。

Method: 提出GSPan框架：1) 用2D高斯溅射将波段级残差细节表示为连续可学习的2D高斯原语；2) 设计双流层次交互（DSHI）架构和空间-光谱交互注意力（SSIA）模块从PAN和MS观测中估计这些原语；3) 将预测的原语渲染为残差细节场并注入上采样MS图像；4) 提出尺度解耦不对称推理（SDAI）策略，在降分辨率下估计原语并在目标分辨率下渲染。

Result: 在QuickBird、GaoFen-2、WorldView-3和WorldView-3-4K数据集上达到SOTA融合性能，SDAI显著加速推理，实现了计算效率与融合质量的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 连续高斯残差表示可作为灵活且尺度解耦的替代方案，突破了固定网格预测的限制，展示了在全色锐化中应用连续表示的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Pansharpening aims to generate high-resolution multispectral (HRMS) images by fusing low-resolution multispectral (LRMS) and panchromatic (PAN) observations. Most existing deep learning methods treat pansharpening as fixed-grid prediction, which limits scale adaptation. To address this, we propose GSPan, a framework that introduces 2D Gaussian Splatting (GS) into pansharpening. Instead of directly predicting pixels, GSPan represents band-wise residual details as continuous and learnable 2D Gaussian primitives. We design a Dual-Stream Hierarchical Interaction (DSHI) architecture with a Spatial-Spectral Interactive Attention (SSIA) module to estimate these primitives from complementary PAN and MS observations. The predicted primitives are rendered as a residual detail field and injected into the upsampled MS image. This continuous representation allows GSPan to render fused images on arbitrary target sampling grids without scale-specific retraining. It further enables a Scale-Decoupled Asymmetric Inference (SDAI) strategy, which estimates primitives at a reduced resolution and renders the fused image at the target resolution for efficient large-scene pansharpening. Experiments on QuickBird, GaoFen-2, WorldView-3, and WorldView-3-4K datasets show that GSPan delivers state-of-the-art fusion performance. Moreover, SDAI markedly accelerates inference, achieving a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency and fusion quality. Our results demonstrate the potential of continuous Gaussian residual representations as a flexible and scale-decoupled alternative to fixed-grid prediction.

</details>


### [61] [ActWorld: From Explorable to Interactive World Model via Action-Aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17730)
*Zhexiao Xiong,Yizhi Song,Hao Kang,Qing Yan,Liming Jiang,Jenson Yang,Zhoujie Fu,Stathi Fotiadis,Angtian Wang,Zichuan Liu,Bo Liu,Yiding Yang,Xin Lu,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActWorld是一个交互式世界模型，解决了现有导航驱动世界模型无法支持物体交互的问题。通过构建10万段带链式推理标注的交互视频数据集，以及引入分层动作感知记忆机制（包含持久记忆库），在保持导航能力的同时实现了高质量的物体交互（如拾取盘子、开门等），显著提升了交互保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型的动作词汇局限于导航（行走、转向、环顾），缺乏与场景中物体的真实交互能力（如拾取盘子、开门、触发物理响应），导致生成的世界仅在视觉上可探索但并非真正可操作。作者指出这一"导航-交互鸿沟"源于两个瓶颈：数据瓶颈（缺乏高质量人工交互标注数据）和记忆瓶颈（现有世界模型的近期偏差压缩机制丢弃了决定物体状态的因果事件转换帧）。

Method: 1) 数据层面：构建100K交互视频数据集，每段视频通过链式推理进行逐块标注。2) 模型层面：提出分层动作感知记忆设计，按交互重要性对历史压缩进行路由；引入持久记忆库，在长程推演中维护事件更新和物体身份令牌。

Result: 实验表明ActWorld在单一模型内同时支持灵活的导航和丰富的物体交互，相比仅导航的基线模型显著提升了交互保真度，且不牺牲视角控制能力。

Conclusion: ActWorld通过解决数据稀缺和记忆瓶颈两大问题，成功将交互式世界模型从纯导航扩展至支持物体交互的范畴，为构建真正可操作的交互世界模型提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Interactive world models aim to simulate environment dynamics under real-time user actions. However, their action vocabulary is largely confined to navigation: most actions correspond to motion (e.g., walk, turn, look around), while interaction with objects in the scene (e.g., pick up plates, open doors, or trigger physical responses) is either absent, restricted to game domains, or relegated to prompt-to-full-video scenarios. The resulting worlds are visually explorable but not truly actionable. In this work, we present ActWorld, an interactive world model that extends prior navigation-centric generators to support mid-rollout object interaction within a chunk-autoregressive framework. We argue that the navigation-interaction gap stems from two bottlenecks. First, a data bottleneck: the lack of human-object interaction data with accurate, dense labels. Second, a memory bottleneck: recency-biased history compression in existing world models discards the event-transition frames that causally determine subsequent object states, leading to an action-forgetting pathology. On the data side, we construct a 100K interaction video dataset, each annotated with per-chunk captions via chain-of-thought reasoning. On the model side, we introduce a hierarchical action-aware memory design that routes history compression by interaction importance, complemented by a persistent memory bank that maintains event-update and object-identity tokens across long rollouts. Experiments show that ActWorld supports both flexible navigation and rich object interaction within a single model, substantially improving interaction fidelity over navigation-only baselines without sacrificing viewpoint control. Project page is available at https://interactwm.github.io/ActWorld.

</details>


### [62] [BrainWorld: A Structural-Prior-Conditioned Generative Model for Whole-Brain 4D fMRI Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17742)
*Junfeng Xia,Wenhao Ye,Junxiang Zhang,Xuanye Pan,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainWorld是一个基于结构先验条件生成的全脑4D fMRI动力学模型，利用sMRI作为解剖上下文引导fMRI生成，在22个数据集上可稳定生成400帧的4D fMRI轨迹，并能通过生成样本增强提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI基础模型主要关注表征学习和下游预测任务，缺乏条件性预测生成能力，无法有效建模全脑4D fMRI动态。

Method: 采用结构先验条件生成框架，将sMRI作为受试者级别的解剖上下文融入去噪过程，指导未来fMRI帧的生成，而非将其视为并行模态。

Result: 在22个涵盖不同队列和脑状态的数据集上，可稳定生成多达400帧的4D fMRI轨迹；通过生成样本增强提升了下游任务性能；学习到可迁移的多模态表征，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: BrainWorld建立了一种条件感知的生成框架，可用于长时程脑动力学建模和多模态表征学习。

Abstract: Whole-brain 4D fMRI generation is valuable for modeling functional brain dynamics, yet existing fMRI foundation models mainly target representation learning and downstream prediction rather than conditional predictive generation. We introduce BrainWorld, a structural-prior-conditioned generative model for whole-brain 4D fMRI dynamics. BrainWorld uses sMRI as subject-level anatomical context to guide future fMRI generation, integrating structural information into the denoising process rather than treating it as a parallel modality. Evaluated on 22 datasets spanning diverse cohorts and brain states, BrainWorld generates stable 4D fMRI trajectories up to 400 frames, improves downstream performance through generated-example augmentation, and learns transferable multimodal representations that outperform baselines. Together, these results establish BrainWorld as a condition-aware generative framework for long-horizon brain dynamics modeling and multimodal representation learning.

</details>


### [63] [LiveStarPro: Proactive Streaming Video Understanding with Hierarchical Memory for Long-Horizon Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17798)
*Zhenyu Yang,Kairui Zhang,Bing Wang,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LiveStarPro，一种面向长时视频流的实时交互助手，通过流式验证解码（SVeD）、流式因果注意力掩码（SCAM）和树状分层记忆（TSHM）三大组件，实现了无需显式静默令牌的自主响应决策、增量视频-语言对齐和高效长程记忆检索。在提出的OmniStarPro基准上，相比现有方法语义正确性提升28.9%，时序误差降低18.2%，推理速度提升1.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线视频大模型在处理连续视频流时，无法同时实现实时响应决策、自主判断响应时机以及保持长时上下文记忆，导致实时性差且在长时间交互中发生严重遗忘。

Method: LiveStarPro包含三个互补组件：(1) 流式验证解码（SVeD），通过单遍困惑度验证确定响应时机，无需显式静默令牌；(2) 流式因果注意力掩码（SCAM），在变长流上强制增量视频-语言对齐的训练策略；(3) 树状分层记忆（TSHM），将驱逐的历史信息组织为事件链的递归记忆架构，实现无限视频流的有效检索。

Result: 在OmniStarPro基准上，LiveStarPro语义正确性提升28.9%，时序误差降低18.2%；流式键值缓存带来1.58倍推理加速。

Conclusion: LiveStarPro在长时间视频流理解任务上显著优于现有方法，解决了实时响应和长时记忆两大核心挑战。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress of Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs), current online architectures still struggle to simultaneously process continuous video streams, decide autonomously when to respond, and preserve long-horizon contextual memory. These obstacles undermine real-time responsiveness and cause severe forgetting throughout prolonged interactions. In this work, we introduce LiveStarPro, a live streaming assistant that is designed for proactive video understanding over long-horizon streams. The design of LiveStarPro rests on three complementary components. The first component is Streaming Verification Decoding (SVeD), an inference framework that identifies the appropriate response timing through single-pass perplexity verification, thereby eliminating the dependency on explicit silence tokens. The second component is Streaming Causal Attention Masks (SCAM), a training strategy that enforces incremental video-language alignment over variable-length streams. The third component is Tree-Structured Hierarchical Memory (TSHM), a recursive memory architecture that organizes evicted historical information into event chains and consequently enables efficient retrieval from effectively unbounded video streams. To facilitate a comprehensive evaluation under realistic online conditions, we further present OmniStarPro, a large-scale benchmark that spans 15 diverse real-world scenarios and that extends to hour-scale streams for the assessment of long-term recall. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LiveStarPro consistently surpasses existing methods, attaining a 28.9% improvement in semantic correctness and an 18.2% reduction in timing error, while its streaming key-value cache further yields a 1.58x inference speedup over the same model without caching. The model and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/sotayang/LiveStarPro.

</details>


### [64] [MaineCoon: Pursuing A Real-Time Audio-Visual Social World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17800)
*Lichen Bai,Tianhao Zhang,Shitong Shao,Dingwei Tan,Qiyu Zhong,Zhengpeng Xie,Haopeng Li,Qinghao Huang,Dandan Shen,Tengjiao Ji,Wei Wang,Peicheng Wu,Yuxuan Zhao,Xiangyu Zhu,Welly Luo,Shurui Yang,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MaineCoon，首个专为社交交互场景设计的实时音视频自回归生成模型，拥有22B参数，可在单GPU上以47.5 FPS速率实现实时流式生成与亚秒级交互，并引入了自重采样、跨模态表示对齐、领域感知偏好优化和强化在线策略蒸馏等创新技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球视频内容越来越多地在社交平台上以交互社交目的被消费，但现有视频生成模型主要面向物理环境模拟或游戏世界探索，缺乏对人类中心社会动态的建模能力，因此需要构建面向社交世界的模型。

Method: 提出MaineCoon模型，采用实时音视频自回归架构（22B参数），引入自重采样、跨模态表示对齐、领域感知偏好优化和强化在线策略蒸馏（ROPD）等技术进行高效稳定训练，并设计首个智能体流式推理框架，通过智能体缓存管理和提示规划支持千秒级甚至更长的生成并缓解漂移。

Result: MaineCoon在单GPU上实现高达47.5 FPS的帧率，支持实时流式生成和亚秒级交互，在高质量、低延迟、长视界音视频自回归建模方面达到新的SOTA性能基准。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为音视频自回归模型设立了新的SOTA基准，还指出了面向下一代AI原生社交平台所需的范式转变，是迈向社交世界模型的第一步。

Abstract: As an increasing majority of global video content is consumed on social platforms for interactive social purposes, video generation models built for social worlds are important but largely overlooked by previous studies. In this work, we define the position of social world models and build a prototype model as the first step towards this goal. While previous world models successfully simulate physical environments or gaming world exploration, they remain fundamentally detached from human-centric social dynamics. To bridge this gap as the first step to social world models, we present MaineCoon, the first real-time audio-visual autoregressive model that has 22B parameters and is capable of real-time streaming generation and sub-second interaction, with a record-breaking frame rate of up to 47.5 FPS, on a single GPU. To the best of our knowledge, MaineCoon is also the first real-time audio-visual generation model specifically optimized for social-interactive applications. To enable efficient and stable training, we introduce several novel techniques into MaineCoon, including self-resampling, cross-modal representation alignment, domain-aware preference optimization, and reinforced online-policy distillation (ROPD). We also design the first agentic streaming inference framework that supports thousand-second-scale or even longer generation while mitigating drift with agentic cache management and prompt planing. These innovations significantly accelerate training while optimizing real-time inference performance. We believe this work not only sets a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance benchmark for high-quality, low-latency, and long-horizon audio-visual autoregressive models, but also points out the paradigm shift desired for next-generation AI-native social platforms.

</details>


### [65] [Million-scale multimodal pollen microscopy with expert-guided foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17809)
*András Biricz,Björn Gedda,Donát Magyar,Antonio Spanu,János Fillinger,Péter Pollner,István Csabai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pollen AI Atlas，一个百万级多模态花粉显微图像资源库，包含来自4个地理来源、4种扫描仪设置、46个分类标签和31个植物科的1,511,390个花粉颗粒检测，并配有机生成的花粉形态学描述。基线实验显示冻结视觉特征达到88.16% top-1准确率，跨区域检索中文本嵌入在图像相似度下降时仍保持鲁棒性（mAP@20 0.811 vs 0.262）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化花粉识别在空气生物学、古生态学和生物多样性监测中仍是瓶颈，因为可扩展系统需要在不同标本制备、扫描仪设置和地理来源下泛化，同时保持孢粉学可解释性。现有方法缺乏大规模、多模态且可解释的花粉影像资源。

Method: 从纯种全切片明场图像出发，通过手动选择每个源切片的代表性样本，经过token级挖掘和过滤，产生1,511,390个花粉颗粒检测（专家验证区域提案精度99.6%）。每个检测配以5个开放权重视觉-语言模型生成的颗粒级形态学描述，由专家验证的孢粉学锚点引导，涵盖萌发孔系统、壁饰纹、形状和大小等结构化描述。

Result: Gemma4提供了最受控的主标题描述集，兼具紧密的长度控制、无泄漏和最强的文本检索性能。基线实验冻结视觉特征达到88.16% top-1准确率；跨区域检索中文本嵌入mAP@20为0.811，而图像相似度仅为0.262，显示文本描述的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Pollen AI Atlas为花粉识别、跨区域域适应和域特定多模态显微学习提供了基准资源，其多模态数据（图像+结构化文本描述）有望弥补传统纯视觉方法的泛化瓶颈，提升花粉自动识别的可解释性和跨域鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated pollen identification from microscopy remains a bottleneck in aerobiology, palaeoecology and biodiversity monitoring, because scalable systems must generalise across specimen preparation, scanner settings and geographic origins while retaining palynological interpretability. To address this gap, we present a million-scale multimodal pollen microscopy resource, Pollen AI Atlas, assembled from pure-species whole-slide bright-field images spanning four geographic origins, four scanner settings and 46 taxon labels across 31 botanical families. Seeded by one manually selected exemplar per source slide, token-level mining and filtering produced 1,511,390 released grain detections with 99.6\% proposal precision in expert-curated test regions. Each detection was paired with machine-generated grain-level morphological captions from five open-weight vision-language models, guided by expert-verified palynological anchors, yielding structured descriptions of aperture systems, wall ornamentation, shape and size. Among the evaluated models, Gemma4 provided the most controlled primary caption set, combining tight length control, no leakage and the strongest text-retrieval performance. Baseline benchmarks with frozen visual features reached 88.16\% top-1 accuracy, while cross-regional retrieval showed that caption-derived text embeddings remained robust when image similarity degraded (mAP@20 0.811 versus 0.262). Released data, annotations, captions, splits, code, and weights provide a benchmark for pollen recognition, cross-regional domain adaptation and domain-specific multimodal microscopy learning.

</details>


### [66] [Human-in-the-Loop Atlas-Based 3D Asset Segmentation for Interactive Content Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17824)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Jakob Hansen,Robin Horst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协同的3D模型分割管线，通过贪婪视角选择、SAM~2交互分割和UV反投影，将3D模型转化为分段的2D参数化图集，支持材质分配、风格迁移等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D资产分割标准依赖具体应用场景且需要用户控制，现有方法难以兼顾自动化效率与用户交互灵活性。

Method: 1) 使用贪婪集覆盖策略选择紧凑的渲染视角；2) 通过SAM~2和Label Studio进行交互式视角分割；3) 将掩码反投影到UV参数化上生成统一的分段图集。

Result: 在8个文化遗产物体上验证了管线的有效性，能够生成可用的分段图集，但也揭示了需人工修正的常见问题：精细结构、空腔及弱外观边界。

Conclusion: 该人机协同管线能有效处理多样化几何形状的3D分割，但精细结构和弱边界区域仍需人工干预。

Abstract: Segmenting 3D assets into meaningful regions remains challenging, especially when segmentation criteria are application-dependent and require user control. We present a human-in-the-loop pipeline for generating a segmented 2D parameterized atlas from a 3D model for interactive media, game, and XR content workflows. Our method first selects a compact set of rendered views using a greedy set cover strategy over sampled surface points, and then supports interactive segmentation of these views with SAM~2 and Label Studio. The resulting masks are back-projected onto the model's UV parameterization to produce a unified segmented atlas that supports downstream production tasks such as segment-wise material assignment, style transfer, and semantic labeling. We assess the pipeline through a demonstration-based technical evaluation on eight cultural heritage objects. The results show that the approach can generate usable segmented atlases across diverse geometries while revealing recurring sources of manual correction, particularly fine structures, cavities, and weak appearance boundaries.

</details>


### [67] [High-Fidelity 3D Geometric Reconstruction of Pelvic Organs from MRI: A Hybrid Deep Learning and Iterative Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17836)
*Hui Wang,Xiaowei Li,Chenxin Zhang,Yifan Feng,Jianwei Zuo,Yumeng Tang,Xiuli Sun,Jianliu Wang,Bing Xie,Jiajia Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合变形形状建模框架，结合深度学习预测与迭代优化，实现从MRI数据中对膀胱、子宫和直肠进行高保真、高质量的3D重建，在几何精度、分割一致性和网格质量上显著优于现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么聚焦于图像分割，要么聚焦于3D模型的下游应用，缺乏对高保真、高质量几何重建的标准化流程，且该过程劳动密集、标准化程度低。

Method: 提出混合变形形状建模框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 几何感知的多级深度学习架构，保持盆腔器官的拓扑一致性；(2) 两阶段摊销优化训练策略，平衡全局形状捕捉与局部表面细化；(3) 全局协同机制——训练阶段迭代优化为深度学习提供监督，推理阶段深度学习快速预测全局器官形态后由迭代优化细化局部表面和网格质量。

Result: 在膀胱、直肠和子宫的重建中，获得了显著更低的Chamfer Distance值和更高的Dice Similarity Coefficient分数；在保持高计算效率的同时，实现了更优的整体体积网格质量；在患者层面，对于最差的10个单元，minSICN和minSIGE的平均值均优于传统几何后处理算法。

Conclusion: 该混合框架在几何保真度上显著优于当前主流的深度学习器官重建模型，为盆腔建模和患者特定分析提供了高效、标准化的高质量几何重建方案。

Abstract: Patient-specific 3D reconstruction of pelvic organ geometry from MRI is important for pelvic floor modeling and downstream patient-specific analysis. However, while previous studies have focused primarily on either image segmentation or downstream use of 3D models, the reconstruction of high-fidelity, high-quality geometries remains labor-intensive and poorly standardized. The study introduced a hybrid deformable shape modeling framework that integrates deep learning prediction with iterative optimization for the reconstruction of the bladder, uterus, and rectum. The framework consists of three core components: a geometry-aware multi-level deep learning architecture that preserves topological consistency of pelvic organs; a two-stage amortized optimization training strategy that balances global shape capture and local surface refinement; and a holistic synergy mechanism--where iterative optimization provides supervision for deep learning during the training phase, and during inference, deep learning rapidly predicts the global organ morphology, followed by iterative optimization to refine local surfaces and mesh quality. This framework demonstrated marked superiority in geometric fidelity than current mainstream deep learning-based organ reconstruction models. For individual anatomical structures, the reconstructed 3D geometries for the bladder, rectum, and uterus achieved significantly lower Chamfer Distance values and higher Dice Similarity Coefficient scores. In addition, while maintaining high computational efficiency, the proposed architecture yielded superior overall volumetric mesh quality. At the patient level, the framework achieved higher mean values for the 10 worst elements for both minSICN and minSIGE compared to traditional geometric post-processing algorithms.

</details>


### [68] [A Quantitative Analysis of Multimodal Biomarkers in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17867)
*Antonio Scardace,Daniele Ravì*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对ADNI数据集中789名受试者的tau-PET、结构MRI、认知评分和APOE4数据进行定量分析，系统研究了阿尔茨海默病多模态生物标志物之间的动态关系，揭示了模态间的冗余性和预测依赖性，并识别出与认知衰退一致的神经退行性轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AD研究中多模态方法日益普及，但分子、结构、临床和遗传生物标志物之间的关系仍不明确，缺乏系统性的定量分析来理解它们之间的动态交互，以改进疾病建模、识别冗余评估并降低患者负担和采集成本。

Method: (A) 量化跨模态互信息和解释方差以评估冗余性和预测依赖性；(B) 检查tau拓扑与结构性萎缩在脑区间的关联，选择信息性ROI；(C) 对tau-认知关联进行统计分解，分为萎缩相关和萎缩独立成分；(D) 识别与认知衰退一致的神经退行性主导轨迹。

Result: 量化了多模态生物标志物之间的冗余性与依赖关系，识别了tau蛋白拓扑结构与脑区萎缩之间的关联模式，将tau-认知关联分解为萎缩相关与萎缩独立成分，并发现了与认知衰退对齐的主导退行性轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究系统表征了AD中跨模态关系，提升了生物标志物的可解释性和选择依据，有助于优化多模态AD研究中的模态选择与数据收集策略。

Abstract: Despite increasing adoption of multimodal approaches in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) research -- aimed at integrating molecular, structural, clinical, and genetic biomarkers to enhance disease characterization -- the relationships among these modalities remain poorly understood. A systematic analysis of their dynamic interaction is essential for improving disease modeling, identifying redundant assessments, and reducing patient burden and acquisition costs. In this paper, we present a quantitative analysis of multimodal AD biomarkers by integrating tau-PET, structural MRI, cognitive scores (MMSE and CDR), and APOE4 data from 789 subjects drawn from the ADNI dataset.
