In our analyses, we (A) quantify cross-modal mutual information and explained variance to assess redundancy and predictive dependencies; (B) examine associations between tau topologies and structural atrophy across brain regions to select informative ROIs; (C) perform a statistical decomposition of the tau-cognition association into atrophy-related and atrophy-independent components; (D) and identify a dominant neurodegenerative trajectory that aligns with cognitive decline. This study provides a systematic characterization of cross-modal relationships, improving the interpretability and selection of biomarkers in AD. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/antonioscardace/Multimodal-AD.

</details>


### [69] [Revisiting Structural Dependency in Autoregressive Multi-Task Table Recognition via Order-Independent Cell-Level Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17874)
*Takaya Kawakatsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构精炼模块，通过非因果注意力生成与顺序无关的单元格特征，实现并行推理，在表格识别任务中提升定位与识别效果，并减少约3倍推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归解码器的多任务表格识别方法中，单元格表示依赖于生成顺序，损害了跨单元格的全局一致性。

Method: 提出结构精炼模块（Structural Refinement Module），通过非因果注意力（non-causal attention）生成与顺序无关的单元格特征，利用全局上下文并行推理单元格内容。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上，单元格定位和端到端识别性能一致提升，推理时间减少约3倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过去耦单元格顺序依赖，兼顾了识别精度与推理效率。

Abstract: Multi-task table recognition jointly addresses table structure prediction, cell localization, and cell content recognition within a unified framework. Existing approaches often rely on autoregressive decoders to generate table structures and reuse their hidden states for cell localization and content recognition. This autoregressive generation process can make cell representations order-dependent, degrading global consistency across cells. This paper proposes a structural refinement module that produces order-independent cell features through non-causal attention. This design enables parallel inference of cell contents while conditioning each cell on global context encoded in the refined features. Experiments on two large datasets demonstrate consistent gains in cell localization and end-to-end recognition, while reducing overall inference time by around threefold.

</details>


### [70] [MoonSplat: Monocular Online Gaussian Splatting with Sim(3) Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17935)
*Guo Pu,Yixuan Han,Haofeng Li,Yao Zhang,Hui Zhou,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoonSplat，一个集成全局Sim(3)优化的鲁棒高效在线体素化3DGS重建框架，通过颜色残差学习策略加速收敛，在相机位姿估计和渲染质量上达到SOTA，并部署了真实的无人机主动重建系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线3DGS方法存在两个关键挑战：缺乏全局优化导致相机位姿估计脆弱，以及在大型场景或长序列场景中优化效率低下。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 提出集成全局Sim(3)优化的在线体素化3DGS重建框架，实现可靠相机跟踪和高效全局闭环（同时对相机位姿和体素化3DGS进行优化）。2) 引入颜色残差学习策略，加速体素化3DGS收敛，提升优化速度和渲染质量。

Result: 在多种室内外数据集上，方法在相机位姿估计精度和渲染质量方面均达到SOTA，同时保持实时效率。进一步的无人机主动重建系统验证了方法的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 本文通过全局Sim(3)优化和颜色残差学习有效解决了在线3DGS的位姿估计脆弱和优化效率低的问题，并在实际无人机系统中验证了实用性。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction from monocular image sequences is a challenging and ongoing research topic. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), leveraging its high-quality real-time rendering capability, empowers online 3D reconstruction to represent dense scenes with enhanced expressiveness, and thus holds great promise for a wide range of applications such as robotics and AR/VR. However, existing online 3DGS methods still suffer from some key challenges: fragile camera pose estimation due to the lack of global optimization, and low optimization efficiency in large-scale or long-sequence scenarios. To address these issues, we propose a robust and efficient online voxelized 3DGS reconstruction framework integrated with global $\text{Sim}(3)$ optimization, which enables reliable camera tracking and efficient global loop closure for both camera poses and voxelized 3DGS. To accelerate the convergence of the voxelized 3DGS, we further introduce a color residual learning strategy, which not only boosts optimization speed but also enhances rendering quality. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both camera pose estimation accuracy and rendering quality, while retaining real-time efficiency. Additionally, we develop and deploy a real-world UAV-based active reconstruction system grounded on our proposed method, validating its robustness and generalizability for practical online 3D reconstruction tasks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/TrickyGo/MoonSplat.

</details>


### [71] [Plug-and-Adapt: Multimodal Coreference Resolution at First Sight with a Pretrained Alignment Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17950)
*Jinghan Wu,Jing Li,Ivor W. Tsang,Xuetao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的多模态共指消解方法，通过预训练的细粒度对齐模型，结合证据理论融合视觉与类别信息，无需目标数据集训练或依赖大模型，在CIN基准上取得SOTA性能提升5.31%（专用方法）和2.12%（大模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCR方法需要目标数据集的（部分）标注数据训练才能应用，限制了直接可用性和泛化能力；而VLLMs参数规模巨大难以部署且多为付费API，不够可及。因此需要一种无需训练、兼容性强的方法。

Method: 首先在视觉-语言对齐数据集上预训练细粒度的文本-视觉上下文对齐模型；然后通过证据理论将视觉线索与类别线索进行相似度聚合，重新将对齐模型适配到MCR任务中。

Result: 在CIN基准上，CoNLL F1相比SOTA专用方法提升5.31%，相比VLLMs提升2.12%；在掩码CIN和VCR-MCR数据集上分别验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效消除对目标数据集训练或大模型的依赖，具备良好的即用性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual information helps resolve ambiguity in coreference resolution, leading to notable performance gains. However, existing Multi-modal Coreference Resolution (MCR) methods require training with (partially) annotated data from the target dataset before they can be applied, preventing their direct usability and raising concerns about generalization. While Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) with billions of parameters offer promising zero-shot capabilities, they remain largely inaccessible. Their massive size limits deployability, and many are only accessible through paid APIs. In this paper, we propose a plug-and-adapt method that strategically adapts a carefully pre-trained \emph{alignment model} for immediate use in MCR tasks, designed to eliminate the need for training on scarce benchmark datasets or relying on resource-intensive VLLMs. Specifically, we first pre-train a fine-grained alignment model between textual and visual contextual information using vision-language alignment datasets. We then repurpose the alignment model to MCR through similarity aggregation by fusing visual and categorical cues with evidence theory, thereby enhancing effectiveness. Experiments on the Coreference Image Narratives (CIN) benchmark dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, achieving a 5.31\% and 2.12\% improvement in CoNLL F1 over SOTA dedicated methods and popular VLLMs, respectively. We further evaluate our method on a masked CIN dataset for robustness testing and on a specially constructed VCR-MCR dataset for generalization assessment, with results confirming both capabilities.

</details>


### [72] [MLLMs Get It Right, Then Get It Wrong: Tracing and Correcting Late-Layer Textual Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17953)
*Xingming Li,Ao Cheng,Qiyao Sun,Xixiang He,Xuanyu Ji,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大模型在视觉-文本冲突时存在“后层文本覆盖”现象：模型早期层能正确基于视觉预测，但最终输出被文本偏见覆盖。通过检测预测变化方向（85%失败转向文本，89%成功转向视觉），提出无需训练的CALRD方法，在推理时恢复被抑制的正确视觉预测，在五个模型上实现最高9.4%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉与文本冲突时倾向于信任文本而非视觉证据，这种文本偏见严重影响了需要视觉定位的应用，但其根本原因尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示这一偏见的内在机制并找到轻量级的解决方案。

Method: 1) 通过分析模型中间层的预测变化，发现"后层文本覆盖"现象；2) 统计发现预测变化方向与正确性存在强相关性；3) 提出CALRD方法，在推理时检测被抑制的自信视觉预测并恢复，无需训练或外部知识。

Result: 在五个不同架构的多模态大模型上，CALRD在冲突基准测试中实现了最高9.4%的绝对性能提升，同时基本保持标准性能不变。

Conclusion: 模型往往已经"知道"正确答案（中间层编码了正确的视觉信息），但未能将其保留到最终输出。通过简单的推理时干预即可恢复这些被覆盖的正确预测，说明文本偏见是后层处理阶段的问题而非视觉编码阶段的问题。

Abstract: When vision contradicts text, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) consistently favor text, even when images provide clear evidence otherwise. This bias poses risks for applications requiring visual grounding, yet its cause remains unclear. In this paper, we uncover a surprising finding: models often get it right initially, forming correct vision-based predictions in their intermediate layers, before changing their minds and favoring text in the final output. We call this "late-layer textual override". The visual information is encoded, it simply does not survive to the output. More intriguingly, we find that how predictions change reveals whether they're correct: 85% of failures shift toward text, while 89% of successes shift toward vision. This directional signature enables a simple but powerful intervention: when we detect a confident visual prediction being suppressed, we restore it. We propose CALRD (Conflict-Aware Layer Reference Decoding), a training-free method that recovers overridden predictions at inference time. Experiments across five MLLMs of varying architectures demonstrate up to 9.4% absolute improvements on conflict benchmarks while largely preserving standard performance, without training or external knowledge. It recovers what the model already knew but failed to preserve.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Visual Cues: CoT-Enhanced Reasoning for Semi-supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17958)
*Yuming Chen,Yuxin Xie,Tao Zhou,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CERS框架，通过引入Chain-of-Thought推理和LLM生成的语义知识，解决半监督医学图像分割中视觉-语义不匹配问题，在边界模糊和语义不一致场景中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督医学图像分割方法过度依赖像素级视觉模式匹配，在视觉相似但诊断结论不同的临床场景（视觉-语义不匹配）中表现不佳，无法捕捉专家使用的诊断逻辑。

Method: 构建包含LLM生成语言推理描述的知识池；提出语义感知参考选择策略（先形态学筛选，再CoT一致性精排）；设计多尺度坐标注意力模块（MCAM）将推理上下文融入解码过程。

Result: 在边界模糊和语义不一致场景下，CERS显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将CoT推理引入医学图像分割可有效缓解视觉-语义鸿沟，提升模型在复杂临床场景中的分割鲁棒性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised medical image segmentation has emerged as a dominant research problem in medical image analysis, mitigating annotation scarcity by leveraging consistency regularization on unlabeled data. However, existing approaches operate predominantly via visual pattern matching, relying heavily on pixel-level similarities. This visual-centric dependency often falters in clinical scenarios characterized by the visual-semantic mismatch, where visually similar lesions warrant distinct diagnostic conclusions, thus failing to capture the underlying diagnostic logic used by experts. To address this, we move beyond visual cues and propose CERS (CoT-Enhanced Reasoning Segmentation), a framework that integrates Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to distinguish pathologically distinct cases. Specifically, we construct a knowledge pool enriched with linguistic reasoning descriptions generated by large language models (LLMs). A semantic-aware reference selection strategy is introduced to identify historical evidence, filtering candidates first by morphology, and then refining them via CoT consistency to eliminate hard negatives. Furthermore, a multi-scale coordinate attention module (MCAM) is designed to effectively fuse this reasoning-derived context into the decoding process. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of CERS against state-of-the-art approaches, particularly in resolving boundary ambiguities and semantic inconsistencies. The code is available at https://github.com/cymasuna/CERS.

</details>


### [74] [Robustness of Similarity-based Positional Encoding Under Rotations: Theoretical Analysis and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17961)
*Andrea Santomauro,Luigi Portinale,Giorgio Leonardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对相似性位置编码（simPE）在旋转扰动下的鲁棒性进行了理论分析与实验验证，证明simPE虽非旋转不变，但在Lipschitz假设下对旋转扰动具有稳定性，并在多个数据集上优于标准位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: simPE在医学影像等几何鲁棒性要求高的应用中表现优异，但其在几何扰动（尤其是旋转）下的理论行为尚未被充分刻画，因此需要严格的理论分析来理解其性质并指导应用。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明simPE一般不具备旋转不变性，然后在温和的Lipschitz假设下证明其对旋转扰动的稳定性，并导出Frobenius范数下的显式扰动界。实验在四个受控数据集（Arrow、Shapes、Digits、FashionMNIST）上进行，训练与验证图像保持标准方向，测试图像施加递增旋转角度。

Result: 在所有数据集上，simPE在旋转条件下的准确率、F1分数、精确率和召回率等指标上均一致优于标准可学习位置编码，尤其是在小到中等旋转角度范围内表现突出，验证了理论稳定性保证。

Conclusion: simPE在旋转扰动下具有理论保证的稳定性，且实验表明其在实际应用中相比标准位置编码具有更强的鲁棒性，特别适用于存在小到中等旋转变化的场景。

Abstract: Positional encoding is a fundamental component of Transformer architectures, as it injects information about the spatial or sequential arrangement of inputs. Among recent alternatives to standard absolute and sinusoidal encodings, similarity-based positional encoding (simPE) has emerged as a flexible framework for representing positional structure through pairwise relations. simPE was originally designed for medical imaging applications, where geometric robustness is especially relevant: small rotations naturally arise during image acquisition, induced by imaging instruments, patient positioning, or slight acquisition misalignments. Despite its empirical promise, the theoretical behavior of simPE under geometric perturbations has not been fully characterized. In this paper, we study the robustness of simPE with respect to rotations, combining formal theoretical analysis with experimental validation. We first show that simPE is generally not rotation-invariant. We then prove that, under mild Lipschitz assumptions on the elementary components, simPE is stable under rotational perturbations and derive explicit perturbation bounds in Frobenius norm. We validate these findings experimentally on four controlled datasets--a synthetic Arrow dataset, a synthetic Shapes dataset (four geometric shape categories), a synthetic Digits dataset, and a benchmark image classification dataset (FashionMNIST)--in which training and validation images are kept in a fixed canonical orientation while test images are subjected to increasing rotation angles. Across all datasets, simPE consistently outperforms standard learned positional encoding in terms of accuracy, F1 score, precision, and recall under rotation, particularly in the small-to-moderate angle regime, corroborating the theoretical stability guarantees.

</details>


### [75] [Reload-Mamba: Hierarchical Anti-Dilution State-Space Modeling for Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17966)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Hsin-Jui Pan,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reload-Mamba 是一种基于 Mamba 状态空间模型的语义分割框架，通过边界监督局部细节先验、类别不确定性感知的 Reload Gate 和分层多级 Reload 机制三大设计，有效缓解了状态空间传播导致的响应稀释问题，在 ADE20K、Cityscapes 和 PASCAL VOC 2012 上取得了优异的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba 状态空间模型虽能实现线性时间的长程建模，但顺序的状态空间传播会削弱对边界和细节敏感的响应，这对于多类语义分割至关重要。现有反稀释架构主要针对二值化任务设计，直接移植到多类分割中效果有限。

Method: 提出三个分割专用设计：(i) 边界监督局部细节先验，利用真实边界掩码训练以识别需响应恢复的区域；(ii) 类别不确定性感知的 Reload Gate，引入预加载辅助头的逐像素类别熵作为额外门控信号；(iii) 分层多级 Reload 机制，在三个解码器层级应用反稀释精炼并自上而下融合。骨干网络采用 ConvNeXt-Tiny 编码器与多尺度解码器，结合四方向 Mamba 扫描与像素级方向注意力。

Result: ADE20K 上达到 47.9% 单尺度 (48.9% 多尺度) mIoU；Cityscapes 上 83.2% 单尺度 mIoU；PASCAL VOC 2012 val 上采用 ResNet-101 + COCO 预训练可达 87.8% mIoU。消融实验表明三大设计均有效贡献，累积较直接移植基线提升 +2.2 mIoU。

Conclusion: Reload-Mamba 通过针对语义分割定制的响应反稀释机制，有效增强了 Mamba 在多类密集预测任务中的边界与细节感知能力，验证了状态空间模型在高分辨率分割中的潜力。

Abstract: Mamba-based state space models offer linear-time long-range modeling for high-resolution dense prediction, but sequential state-space propagation can attenuate boundary-sensitive and detail-sensitive responses that are critical in multi-class semantic segmentation. We propose Reload-Mamba, a semantic segmentation framework that addresses this propagation-induced response dilution through three segmentation-specific designs: (i) a boundary-supervised local detail prior that is explicitly trained with ground-truth boundary masks to identify regions requiring response restoration; (ii) a class-uncertainty-aware Reload Gate that incorporates per-pixel class entropy from a pre-reload auxiliary head as an additional gating signal, a formulation that is informative only under multi-class dense prediction; and (iii) a hierarchical multi-level Reload mechanism that applies anti-dilution refinement at three decoder levels and fuses the restored representations top-down. Built upon a ConvNeXt-Tiny encoder with a multi-scale decoder and four-directional Mamba scanning with pixel-wise directional attention, Reload-Mamba achieves 47.9% single-scale (48.9% multi-scale) mIoU on ADE20K and 83.2% single-scale mIoU on Cityscapes. With ResNet-101 + COCO pre-training under the standard DeepLab-style protocol, Reload-Mamba reaches 87.8% mIoU on PASCAL VOC 2012 val. Controlled ablations show that each of the three segmentation-specific designs contributes beyond a direct port of the prior anti-dilution architecture proposed for binarization, cumulatively improving over the direct-port baseline by +2.2 mIoU on ADE20K.

</details>


### [76] [SegDINO: Introducing Multi-Scale Structure into DINO for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17972)
*Sicheng Yang,Hongqiu Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Sixiang Chen,Qiuxia Yang,Yize Mao,Guang Yang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegDINO是一个高效的图像分割框架，通过DINOv3骨干网络结合轻量级尺度建模（Token Pyramid Adaptation和Scale-Aware Decoding），在多个基准上实现SOTA性能。同时提出了包含284名患者的胰腺肿瘤CT数据集PanCT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将DINO模型应用于图像分割时，通常依赖带有复杂上采样的重型解码器，带来大量参数和计算开销。作者观察到为DINO特征引入尺度信息远比增加解码器容量更重要，因此希望设计一个高效的分割框架。

Method: 提出SegDINO框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA)——将DINO中间特征重组为伪多尺度层级结构；(2) Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD)——实现高效的尺度内细化和自顶向下的多尺度传播。此外，构建了PanCT数据集（284名患者的胰腺肿瘤CT数据）。

Result: 在PanCT和三个公开基准数据集上，SegDINO取得了state-of-the-art的结果，同时保持了高计算效率。

Conclusion: 为DINO特征引入尺度建模比增加解码器容量更关键，SegDINO通过轻量级尺度感知设计实现了高效且精准的医学与自然图像分割。

Abstract: Self-supervised DINO models provide strong transferable visual representations, yet applying them directly to image segmentation remains challenging. Existing approaches commonly rely on heavy decoders with complex upsampling, introducing substantial parameter and computational overhead. We observe that introducing scale into DINO features is far more critical than increasing decoder capacity. In this work, we present SegDINO, an efficient segmentation framework that integrates a DINOv3 backbone with lightweight scale modeling. SegDINO introduces Token Pyramid Adaptation (TPA) to reorganize intermediate DINO features into a pseudo multi-scale hierarchy, and Scale-Aware Decoding (SAD) for efficient intra-scale refinement and top-down multi-scale propagation. We further curate PanCT, a new CT dataset containing 284 patients with expert-annotated pancreatic tumors, to assess SegDINO's ability to handle difficult small-lesion cases. Extensive experiments on PanCT and three public benchmarks demonstrate that SegDINO achieves state-of-the-art results with high efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/segdino_v2.

</details>


### [77] [Gaussian Light Field Splatting: A Physical Prior-Driven Vision Transformer for Unsupervised Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17985)
*Yuhan Chen,Wenxuan Yu,Guofa Li,Fuchen Li,Kunyang Huang,Yicui Shi,Ying Fang,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GLFS，一种基于高斯光场泼溅的视觉Transformer，通过将连续物理光照建模（各向异性高斯函数叠加）融入Transformer架构，并引入物理引导的注意力偏置、色向量角度损失和亮度边缘损失，解决了非均匀光照下的局部曝光失衡和色彩失真问题，在暗光图像增强上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督暗光增强方法在复杂非均匀光照下存在局部曝光失衡和色彩失真；多数视觉Transformer缺乏对光照退化物理先验的显式建模机制。

Method: 1) 场景光照用各向异性高斯基函数叠加表示；2) 在自注意力中引入物理引导偏置，自适应推断空间增益场；3) 提出色向量角度损失和亮度边缘损失，分别约束色调一致性和局部细节结构保真度。

Result: 大量消融实验和定量评估表明，GLFS在光照校正和细节保持方面具有明显优势，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GLFS为暗光图像增强提供了新的表示范式，通过连续物理光照建模与Transformer的有效融合，显著提升了复杂光照下的恢复质量。

Abstract: Existing unsupervised low-light image enhancement methods often encounter local exposure imbalance and color distortion under complex non-uniform illumination. In addition, most Vision Transformers lack an explicit mechanism for modeling the physical priors of illumination degradation. To address these limitations, we propose GLFS, a Gaussian light field splatting-based Vision Transformer that integrates continuous physical illumination modeling from Gaussian splatting into the Transformer architecture. In GLFS, scene illumination is represented by a superposition of anisotropic Gaussian basis functions. Physics-guided biases are introduced into self-attention to adaptively infer a spatial gain field, enabling accurate and uniform restoration under complex illumination. To reduce color bias and structural degradation during enhancement, a color-vector angular loss and a luminance-edge loss are further developed. These losses enforce hue consistency and improve the structural fidelity of local details. Extensive ablation studies and quantitative evaluations show that GLFS provides clear advantages in illumination correction and detail preservation. It achieves state-of-the-art performance and offers a new representation paradigm for low-light image enhancement.

</details>


### [78] [Recover Semantics First, Generate Better: Improved Latent Modeling for 3D MRI Reconstruction and Cross-Contrast Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17989)
*Yonghao Chen,Sicheng Yang,Rui Tang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义优先的潜在空间建模框架（RSF），用于3D MRI重建与跨对比度合成。通过潜在协调编码器（LHE）捕获全局解剖依赖，语义恢复模块（SRB）注入高维先验以增强潜在空间对比度可分离性，以及解剖感知频率损失（AFL）保留诊断相关高频结构，解决了现有压缩架构中长程解剖一致性不足、语义信息丢失和重建过平滑的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对比度MRI采集耗时且成本高，生成模型虽可进行跨对比度合成，但3D MRI体积庞大导致直接在像素空间计算不可行，需先压缩到潜在空间。然而现有压缩架构存在三个关键问题：长程解剖一致性保持不足、临床语义信息丢失、优化目标导致重建过平滑，这些问题严重影响了后续生成模型的性能。

Method: 提出语义优先的潜在建模框架RSF，包含三个核心组件：1）潜在协调编码器（LHE）用于捕获全局解剖依赖关系，确保体积表示的一致性；2）语义恢复模块（SRB）从自监督语义教师模型中注入高维先验，增强潜在空间中不同对比度之间的可分离性；3）解剖感知频率损失（AFL）自适应保留诊断相关的高频结构信息。

Result: 在两个公开多对比度MRI数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建保真度和跨对比度合成质量方面均取得了一致性提升。

Conclusion: 通过语义优先的潜在空间建模，有效解决了3D MRI压缩中的解剖一致性、语义退化及过平滑问题，显著提升了重建与合成的质量。

Abstract: Multi-contrast magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provides complementary information for clinical diagnosis. However, acquiring all MRI sequences is often time-consuming and costly. Recent generative models perform cross-contrast synthesis to address this issue by inferring absent contrasts from the available ones. Nevertheless, synthesizing 3D MRI presents significant challenges. Due to the massive volume sizes, operating directly in the pixel space is computationally prohibitive; therefore, a common approach is to first compress the 3D volumes into a latent space and subsequently train generative models in that space. We observe that existing compression architectures face several critical issues: they under-preserve long-range anatomical coherence, discard clinically meaningful semantics, and rely on optimization objectives that lead to over-smoothed reconstructions. Ultimately, these shortcomings compromise the performance of subsequent generative models. In this work, we propose a semantics-first latent modeling framework for 3D MRI reconstruction and cross-contrast synthesis. Specifically, we introduce a Latent Harmonization Encoder (LHE) to capture global anatomical dependencies, ensuring coherent volumetric representations. To mitigate semantic degradation during latent compression, we further design a Semantic Recovery Block (SRB) that injects high-level priors from a self-supervised semantic teacher, enhancing contrast-aware separability in the latent space. Additionally, we propose an Anatomy-aware Frequency Loss (AFL) to adaptively preserve diagnostically relevant high-frequency structures. Extensive experiments on two public multi-contrast MRI datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in reconstruction fidelity and cross-contrast synthesis quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/script-Yang/RSF.

</details>


### [79] [AIGS-Net: Compact Illumination Field Modeling via 2D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17998)
*Yuhan Chen,Kunyang Huang,Fuchen Li,Zhuohan Qin,Guofa Li,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AIGS-Net，一种超轻量级自适应照明高斯溅射网络，仅需约40个可学习参数即可实现快速低光照图像增强，通过输入自适应的2D高斯溅射照明场、零参数多尺度上下文编码模块以及噪声抑制和色彩校正机制，在LOL和LSRW基准上取得了增强质量与推理效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光照图像增强方法在照明场建模的表征能力与计算复杂度之间存在瓶颈。

Method: AIGS-Net构建输入自适应的2D高斯溅射照明场，通过相对亮度统计动态调制高斯基函数的不透明度，并采用有序alpha合成渲染空间变化的照明补偿；引入零参数非线性多尺度上下文编码模块提取低频结构和局部对比度线索；集成噪声掩码估计、锁定单通道Gamma映射、跨通道一致性正则化和目标颜色对齐约束。

Result: 在LOL和LSRW基准上，AIGS-Net在细节恢复和色彩保真度方面表现优异，仅需约40个可学习参数。

Conclusion: AIGS-Net在增强质量与极端推理效率之间实现了有效权衡。

Abstract: Existing low-light image enhancement methods often face a bottleneck between the representation capacity of illumination-field modeling and computational complexity. To address this issue, this paper proposes an Adaptive Illumination Gaussian Splatting Network (AIGS-Net), an ultra-lightweight architecture for fast low-light enhancement. Unlike conventional static priors, AIGS-Net constructs an input-adaptive 2D Gaussian Splatting illumination field. The opacity of Gaussian basis functions is dynamically modulated by relative luminance statistics of the input image, and spatially varying illumination compensation is rendered through ordered alpha compositing. To guide adaptive illumination compensation efficiently, a zero-parameter nonlinear multiscale contextual encoding module is introduced to extract low-frequency structures and local contrast cues without additional convolutional weights. To suppress noise amplification and sensor-induced color bias, AIGS-Net integrates noise-mask estimation, locked single-channel Gamma mapping, cross-channel consistency regularization, and target color-alignment constraints. Experiments on LOL and LSRW benchmarks show that AIGS-Net improves detail recovery and color fidelity while requiring only approximately 40 learnable parameters, achieving an effective trade-off between enhancement quality and extreme inference efficiency.

</details>


### [80] [PhaseWin: An Efficient Search Algorithm for Faithful Visual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18008)
*Zihan Gu,Ruoyu Chen,Junchi Zhang,Li Liu,Xiaochun Cao,Hua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseWin是一种高效的视觉归因算法，通过分阶段窗口搜索将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，在保持接近贪婪搜索忠实度的同时大幅减少模型评估次数，在多项视觉任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有忠实视觉归因方法需要将问题建模为有序子集搜索，但穷举搜索成本指数级，而广泛使用的贪婪搜索仍需二次方数量的模型评估（每步需重新评分所有剩余候选区域），计算效率低下。

Method: PhaseWin将贪婪区域选择重组为分阶段窗口搜索过程：交替进行全局候选筛选、自适应剪枝和局部窗口细化，避免每步重新评估完整候选集，同时在单调证据累积假设下保证接近贪婪搜索的忠实度。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、视觉定位和图像描述等任务中，PhaseWin相比所有对比归因方法，以最少的正向传播次数达到了高忠实度，实证实现了从O(n²)到O(n)的复杂度降低。

Conclusion: PhaseWin通过分阶段窗口搜索策略，在保持归因忠实度的前提下将计算复杂度从二次降至线性，为高效忠实视觉归因提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual attribution is a fundamental tool for interpreting modern vision and vision-language models, particularly when their decisions must be inspected, diagnosed, or audited. Its goal is to explain how a model's decision depends on local regions of the visual input, typically by assigning an importance ordering over candidate image regions. Given an image partitioned into $n$ regions, faithful attribution can be cast as an ordered subset-search problem, in which progressively inserting the selected regions should recover the target model response as early as possible. Exhaustive search over region subsets incurs exponential cost, while the widely used greedy search still requires a quadratic number of model evaluations, because every selection step rescores all remaining candidates. We propose PhaseWin, an efficient subset-search algorithm for faithful visual attribution. PhaseWin reorganizes greedy region selection into a phased window-search procedure: rather than re-evaluating the full candidate set at every step, it alternates between global candidate screening, adaptive pruning, and localized window refinement, while preserving the essential region-ranking behavior of greedy search. We analyze PhaseWin under monotone evidence-accumulation conditions and show that, under feature-level structural assumptions, it attains controllable linear evaluation complexity together with near-greedy faithfulness guarantees. Extensive experiments on image classification, object detection, visual grounding, and image captioning show that, among all compared attribution methods, PhaseWin reaches high faithfulness with the fewest forward passes, empirically realizing the predicted reduction from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$. The code is available at https://github.com/Qihuai27/phasewin-va.

</details>


### [81] [When LLMs Analyze Scars: From Images to Clinically-Meaningful Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18063)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScaFE框架，将大语言模型作为知识驱动的特征工程师，通过生成可执行代码提取临床可解释特征，解决医学图像分类中数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类面临数据稀缺困境（标注成本高、隐私限制、疾病罕见），尤其在病理瘢痕分类中，区分瘢痕疙瘩与增生性瘢痕需要专家知识且标注图像极度有限。

Method: 利用LLM基于瘢痕评估标准（如Vancouver Scar Scale）生成确定性Python特征提取代码，将高维图像转化为低维临床可解释表征，LLM作为知识驱动的特征工程师而非端到端分类器。

Result: 在瘢痕分类实验中，该方法在有限数据条件下持续优于端到端深度学习基线和使用LLM作为黑箱分类器的方案。

Conclusion: 该范式具备数据高效性（知识获取与统计学习解耦）、隐私保护（图像本地处理）和可解释性（基于临床推理的显式特征），为将LLM整合到数据高效且临床透明的医学AI系统提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Medical image classification faces a fundamental dilemma: while deep learning models achieve remarkable performance at scale, real-world clinical scenarios often suffer from severe data scarcity due to annotation costs, privacy constraints, and disease rarity. This challenge is particularly pronounced in pathological scar classification, where differentiating keloids from hypertrophic scars requires subtle expert knowledge and labeled images are extremely limited. We propose a novel paradigm that repositions large language models (LLMs) as knowledge-driven feature engineers rather than end-to-end classifiers. We call this framework ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering). Our key insight is that LLMs encode rich medical knowledge that can be externalized as executable feature extraction code, enabling the transformation of high-dimensional images into low-dimensional, clinically interpretable representations. Specifically, we prompt an LLM with established scar assessment criteria to generate deterministic Python code that extracts features aligned with clinical scoring systems such as the Vancouver Scar Scale. Our approach offers three key advantages: (1) data efficiency, achieving robust performance with limited training samples by decoupling knowledge acquisition from statistical learning; (2) privacy preservation, as raw images are processed locally without exposure to external LLMs; and (3) interpretability, through explicit features grounded in clinical reasoning. Extensive experiments on scar classification demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms end-to-end deep learning baselines or using LLMs as black-box classifiers under limited data conditions, establishing a promising direction for integrating LLMs into data-efficient and clinically transparent medical AI systems.

</details>


### [82] [HLS-GPT: A Generative Pretrained Transformer (GPT) for Continental-Scale NASA Harmonized Landsat and Sentinel-2 (HLS) Reflectance Reconstruction Across All Bands on Arbitrary Dates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18115)
*Junjie Li,Hankui K. Zhang,David P. Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HLS-GPT，一个大规模生成式预训练Transformer模型，用于重建NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 (HLS) 30米地表反射率时间序列。该模型采用分层Transformer架构，处理Landsat和Sentinel-2的不同光谱波段配置，在单像素12个月时间序列上操作。使用美国本土超过25万像素、九年的HLS数据训练，通过随机裁剪和掩码策略（掩码50%有效观测）学习重建被掩码的反射率值。在超过6.2万独立测试像素上评估，所有波段RMSE低于0.026，即使掩码10%-50%观测时性能退化很小（全波段RMSE低于0.028），并在9个独立HLS瓦片上优于传统方法和NASA-IBM Prithvi模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在Landsat和Sentinel-2反射率时间序列重建中存在光谱覆盖受限、地理可扩展性有限或基于图块设计且时间上下文短等问题，无法实现全波段、任意日期、任意像素位置的高质量重建。

Method: 提出HLS-GPT模型：1）采用分层Transformer架构处理不同卫星的波段配置差异；2）在单像素12个月时间序列上操作；3）使用美国本土>25万像素、九年的HLS数据训练；4）采用随机裁剪和掩码策略（掩码50%有效观测）训练模型重建被掩码值；5）支持所有HLS波段、任意日期和任意像素位置的重建。

Result: 超过6.2万独立测试像素评估：留一观测法评估所有波段RMSE低于0.026，可见光波段相对RMSE低于35%，其他波段低于13%；红边波段误差与红和近红外相当；掩码10%-50%观测时全波段RMSE低于0.028；在9个独立HLS瓦片上优于两种传统方法和Prithvi模型。

Conclusion: HLS-GPT在大规模地理范围、全光谱波段和任意时间点的反射率重建上表现鲁棒，能处理复杂作物物候和稀疏不规则观测，且对观测缺失具有良好容忍性，优于现有传统方法和同类深度学习模型。

Abstract: Recent deep learning methods for Landsat and Sentinel-2 reflectance time series reconstruction remain limited by restricted spectral coverage, limited geographic scalability, or patch-based designs with short temporal contexts. We present HLS-GPT, a large-scale generative pretrained Transformer model for reconstructing NASA Harmonized Landsat Sentinel-2 30 m surface reflectance for all bands, any date, and any pixel location. HLS-GPT uses a hierarchical Transformer architecture to handle the different spectral band configurations of Landsat and Sentinel-2 and operates on single-pixel 12-month time series. To capture geographic and seasonal variability, the model was trained with nine years of HLS time series from more than 0.25 million training pixels across the conterminous United States. A random cropping and masking strategy extracts 12-month periods with varying start dates across epochs, masks 50% of valid observations, and trains the model to reconstruct the masked reflectance values from the remaining observations. Evaluation using more than 62,000 independent test pixels shows robust reconstruction under diverse land surface conditions, including complex crop phenology and sparse, irregular observations. Leave-one-observation-out evaluation achieved reconstruction RMSE below 0.026 for all HLS spectral bands, with relative RMSE below 35% for visible bands and below 13% for other bands. Red-edge band errors were comparable to red and near-infrared errors despite the absence of red-edge bands on Landsat. Sensitivity analyses that randomly masked 10% to 90% of test observations showed only modest degradation when 10% to 50% of observations were masked, with all-band RMSE below 0.028. Image reconstruction over nine independent 109 by 109 km CONUS HLS tiles further demonstrates that HLS-GPT outperforms two conventional methods and the NASA-IBM Prithvi model.

</details>


### [83] [Predicting Immune Biomarkers with MultiModal Mixture-of-Expert Pathology Foundation Models Empowers Precision Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18123)
*Tianyu Liu,Ziqing Wang,Zhaokang Liang,Tong Ding,Peter Humphrey,Lorraine Colón-Cartagena,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Kenneth Tou En Chang,Mohamed Kahila,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Tinglin Huang,Rex Ying,Kaize Ding,Faisal Mahmood,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixTIME是一个多模态基础模型，采用混合专家（MoE）架构整合多种病理基础模型（仅图像UNIv2、图像文本CONCHv1.5、图像转录组STPath），实现从HE全切片图像到多重免疫荧光（mIF）蛋白表达的像素级和切片级预测，在17个蛋白标志物上达到SOTA，并显著提升空间域识别、生存预测和AI病理报告生成等下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测肿瘤免疫微环境（TIME）生物标志物的方法局限于单一图像模态，存在分辨率不足以及临床和生物学信息利用不完整的问题，亟需一种整合多模态互补信息的高分辨率预测框架。

Method: 采用混合专家（MoE）架构，集成三种不同模态的病理基础模型（图像仅UNIv2、图像文本CONCHv1.5、图像转录组STPath），通过学习式路由器动态加权各专家贡献，并设计了分布与趋势感知的损失函数进行训练。

Result: 在两个不同规模的数据集上，MixTIME在17个蛋白标志物上取得SOTA相关性指标；预测的mIF谱显著增强了空间域识别、生存预测和多中心专家验证的AI病理报告生成等下游任务；还能实现临床时间点蛋白表达动态追踪，揭示与耐药和免疫抑制相关的蛋白基因互作模式。

Conclusion: MixTIME为计算病理学中的多模态生物标志物发现和临床转化提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Predicting immune biomarkers associated with the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME) is critical for advancing precision oncology, yet existing approaches are largely limited to single image modalities and suffer from insufficient resolution and incomplete utilization of complementary clinical and biological information. Here we introduce MixTIME, a multimodal foundation model that leverages a mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture to integrate pathology foundation models trained across distinct modalities: image only (UNIv2), image text (CONCHv1.5), and image transcriptomic (STPath) representations for pixel-level and slide-level prediction of multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) protein expression from hematoxylin and eosin (HE) whole-slide images. MixTIME employs a learnable router to dynamically weight expert contributions and is trained with a distribution- and tendency-aware loss function. Benchmarked on two datasets of different scales, MixTIME achieves state-of-the-art performance across 17 protein markers as measured by correlation metrics. The predicted mIF profiles substantially enhance downstream tasks, including spatial domain identification, survival prediction, and AI-assisted pathology report generation validated by expert pathologists from multiple institutes across the world. Furthermore, MixTIME enables longitudinal tracking of protein expression dynamics across clinical time points and reveals protein gene interaction patterns linked to drug resistance and immune suppression in tumor microenvironments. Collectively, MixTIME provides a scalable framework for multimodal biomarker discovery and clinical translation in computational pathology.

</details>


### [84] [Neural Tree Reconstruction for the Open Forest Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18153)
*Marissa Ramirez de Chanlatte,Arjun Rewari,Trevor Darrell,Derek J. N. Young*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open Forest Observatory (OFO) 是一个跨大学合作项目，旨在通过低成本无人机航拍和开源工具构建森林地理空间数据库，当前使用经典运动恢复结构（SfM）方法生成3D树图，但存在伪影、细节缺失及林下可见性差等问题，计划引入NeRF等先进3D重建技术以提高质量，从而支持森林生态与气候应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OFO使用的经典SfM方法生成的3D树图存在伪影、细节不足，且在林下区域因航拍影像可见性受限而重建困难，这些误差可能传导至下游科学任务（如野火模拟），因此需要探索更鲁棒、更高质量的3D重建方法。

Method: 论文探讨将Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) 等先进3D重建技术整合到OFO数据处理流程中，以替代当前经典SfM方法，并规划未来支持更前沿的3D视觉模型。

Result: 指出NeRF方法相比SfM能生成更高质量的结果，对稀疏视角更鲁棒，且支持数据驱动的先验知识，可有效改善OFO数据集中的3D树图重建质量。

Conclusion: 高质量3D重建对于森林应用（如再造林优先级评估、野火风险降低、碳汇监测）至关重要，将NeRF等先进技术纳入OFO框架可显著提升数据可用性和科学价值。

Abstract: The Open Forest Observatory (OFO) is a collaboration across universities and other partners to make low-cost forest mapping accessible to ecologists, land managers, and the general public. The OFO is building both a database of geospatial forest data as well as open-source methods and tools for forest mapping by uncrewed aerial vehicle. Such data are useful for a variety of climate applications including prioritizing reforestation efforts, informing wildfire hazard reduction, and monitoring carbon sequestration. In the current iteration of the OFO's forest map database, 3D tree maps are created using classical structure-from-motion techniques. This approach is prone to artifacts, lacks detail, and has particular difficulty on the forest floor where the input data (overhead imagery) has limited visibility. These reconstruction errors can potentially propagate to the downstream scientific tasks (e.g. a wildfire simulation.) Advances in 3D reconstruction, including methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), produce higher quality results that are more robust to sparse views and support data-driven priors. We explore ways to incorporate NeRFs into the OFO dataset, outline future work to support even more state-of-the-art 3D vision models, and describe the importance of high-quality 3D reconstructions for forestry applications.

</details>


### [85] [ReAge3D: Re-Aging 3D Faces with View Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18156)
*Libing Zeng,Li Ma,Mingming He,Ning Yu,Paul Debevec,Nima Khademi Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的3D人脸老化编辑框架，通过2D扩散模型DiffReaging和中心向外传播策略实现多视图一致的老化编辑，生成高保真、身份保持的老化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法适用于粗粒度语义变化，但在人脸老化任务中，跨视图的细微不一致会导致与年龄相关的重要细节被过度平滑，缺乏精细可控的老化编辑能力。

Method: 1) 提出DiffReaging，一个在合成图像对上训练的2D扩散老化模型；2) 提出中心向外传播策略：从老化后的正面枢轴视图出发，通过warping和Masked-DiffReaging过程重建其余视图；3) Masked-DiffReaging通过在扩散每一步注入已有内容，确保重建区域与现有像素一致；4) 用一致的视图集监督3D表示的优化。

Result: 在视觉和定量指标上均优于现有3D编辑技术，能实现平滑、细粒度的人脸年龄变换控制。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了3D人脸老化编辑中的多视图一致性问题，生成了细节丰富、身份保持的老化结果，实现了对年龄变换的精细控制。

Abstract: We present a novel framework for realistic and controllable 3D face re-aging which produces highly detailed, identity-preserving results. Existing 3D editing methods, while effective for coarse semantic changes, are not well suited for re-aging, as even small inconsistencies across re-aged 2D views can lead to over-smoothing of subtle but perceptually important age-related details. To address this challenge, we first introduce a 2D diffusion-based re-aging model, DiffReaging, trained on synthetically generated image pairs. We further propose a center-out editing propagation strategy that leverages this re-aging model to reconstruct multi-view-consistent re-aged images. Specifically, starting from a re-aged frontal pivot view, we reconstruct the remaining views through warping and our proposed Masked-DiffReaging process. By injecting existing content at every step of the diffusion process, Masked-DiffReaging ensures that the reconstructed regions remain coherent with existing pixels. The resulting consistent set of re-aged views supervises the optimization of the re-aged 3D representation. Our method outperforms existing 3D editing techniques both visually and quantitatively, enabling smooth, fine-grained control over age transformations in 3D face models.

</details>


### [86] [EgoCS-400K: An Egocentric Gameplay Dataset for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18180)
*Rongjin Guo,Dong Liang,Yuhao Liu,Fang Liu,Tianyu Huang,Gerhard P. Hancke,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoCS-400K，一个基于反恐精英（CS/CS2）职业比赛回放的大规模、以重放为基础的自我中心世界模型数据集，包含40万+第一人称视频、1万小时游戏时长，并提供动作、状态、事件、视角等多模态时间对齐标注，可用于动作条件未来预测、状态感知场景推演等交互式视觉建模任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频生成向交互式世界建模转变需要时间对齐的动作-视频-语言轨迹数据，但现有网络视频缺乏可执行动作和可靠状态，机器人数据成本高且场景多样性有限，模拟器缺乏大规模人类交互轨迹。

Method: 利用公开的职业CS/CS2比赛回放（demo），提取玩家状态、视角方向、移动、键盘/按键输入、视角变化、武器使用、游戏事件、回合级上下文，并从相同轨迹渲染干净的第一人称视频，构建了EgoCS-400K数据集。

Result: EgoCS-400K包含超过40万第一人称视频、1万小时游戏时长、1000+场比赛、4万+回合，覆盖13张地图和每回合10个玩家视角，支持动作条件未来预测、状态与事件感知场景推演、回放接地字幕生成、智能体自我中心动作理解等任务。

Conclusion: EgoCS-400K通过大规模连接视觉观察与人类动作、相机运动、游戏状态和事件，成为被动网络视频、可控游戏模拟与昂贵真实世界具身数据之间的实用桥梁。

Abstract: The shift from video generation to interactive world modeling places new demands on data: beyond captioned videos, world models require temporally aligned video-action-language trajectories grounded in the actions, camera motion, states, and events that drive future scene changes. However, such data is difficult to obtain at scale. Web video datasets offer broad visual coverage but lack executable actions and reliable states; robotic datasets provide action and state supervision but are costly and limited in scene diversity; and existing simulators often lack large-scale human-driven interaction trajectories. In this paper, we introduce EgoCS-400K, a large-scale replay-grounded egocentric Counter-Strike dataset for world models, built from public professional CS and CS2 match demos that preserve human gameplay trajectories and enable parsing, replaying, rendering, and temporal alignment. We extract player states, view directions, movements, keyboard/button inputs, view-angle changes, weapon usage, game events, and round-level context, and render clean first-person videos from the same trajectories. EgoCS-400K contains over 400,000 first-person videos and 10,000 hours of gameplay from more than 1,000 matches and 40,000 rounds, covering 13 maps and 10 player viewpoints per round. It supports a range of interactive visual modeling tasks, including action-conditioned future prediction, state- and event-aware scene rollout, replay-grounded captioning, and agent egocentric action understanding. By connecting visual observations with human actions, camera motion, game states, and events at scale, EgoCS-400K serves as a practical bridge between passive web videos, controllable game simulation, and costly real-world embodied data.

</details>


### [87] [EventDrive: Event Cameras for Vision-Language Driving Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18242)
*Dongyue Lu,Rong Li,Ao Liang,Lingdong Kong,Wei Yin,Lai Xing Ng,Benoit R. Cottereau,Camille Simon Chane,Wei Tsang Ooi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventDrive是一个大规模基准和模型套件，通过多视界事件金字塔和时序混合专家模块统一事件流、RGB帧和语言监督，在自动驾驶的感知、理解、预测和规划四个核心维度上展示了事件传感对时序精度、运动感知和鲁棒性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件感知视觉语言模型仅局限于通用感知任务，未能揭示事件传感如何在完整驾驶循环中贡献于推理和决策过程，缺乏涵盖感知、理解、预测和规划全链路的统一基准。

Method: 构建EventDrive基准，统一事件流、RGB帧和语言监督，覆盖四个核心维度；提出EventDrive-VLM模型，包含多视界事件金字塔和时序-视界混合专家模块，用于自适应编码和融合异步事件与帧信息以支持下游推理。

Result: 在多样化任务上的综合评估表明，事件流在时序精度、运动感知和鲁棒性方面带来了实质性提升。

Conclusion: 事件传感能够将运动感知能力置于驾驶智能的中心位置，有效补充传统帧基感知在模糊、眩光和快速运动场景下的不足。

Abstract: Event cameras sense the world through asynchronous brightness changes with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, offering motion fidelity far beyond frame-based sensors and capturing temporal structure that conventional exposures often miss. These properties make events a powerful complement to RGB in autonomous driving, especially under blur, glare, and rapid motion, where frame-based perception can become unreliable. However, existing event-aware vision-language models remain limited to generic perception and do not reveal how event sensing contributes to reasoning and decision-making across the full driving loop. We present EventDrive, a large-scale benchmark and model suite that unifies event streams, RGB frames, and language supervision across four core dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Prediction, and Planning, covering captions, structured QA, grounding, motion-state recognition, trajectory forecasting, and planning tasks. Building on this foundation, EventDrive-VLM introduces a multi-horizon event pyramid and a temporal-horizon mixture-of-experts module to adaptively encode and fuse asynchronous and frame-based information for downstream reasoning. Comprehensive evaluation across diverse tasks shows that event streams provide substantial gains in temporal precision, motion awareness, and robustness, bringing event sensing into the center of driving intelligence.

</details>


### [88] [Unified Multimodal Autoregressive Modeling with Shared Context-Visual Tokenizer is Key to Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18249)
*Wujian Peng,Lingchen Meng,Yuxuan Cai,Xianwei Zhuang,Yuhuan Yang,Rongyao Fang,Chenfei Wu,Junyang Lin,Zuxuan Wu,Shuai Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAR提出统一自回归框架，使用单一离散视觉分词器桥接视觉理解与生成，通过多级特征融合、无查找量化、并行位预测和扩散解码器，在图像生成和编辑任务上达到SOTA，同时保持多模态理解竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态建模方法依赖两个不同的视觉分词器，导致表征空间分裂，阻碍了真正的统一建模。需要一种单一分词器架构来实现理解与生成的共享上下文。

Method: 1) 单一离散视觉分词器：适配预训练视觉编码器，采用多级特征融合和无查找位量化方案；2) 并行位预测：联合预测空间分组的多级视觉编码，缩短序列长度并加速生成；3) 基于扩散的视觉解码器：从离散视觉标记解码高保真图像；4) 三阶段训练：大规模预训练+监督微调+强化学习。

Result: 在图像生成和图像编辑任务上达到SOTA性能，在多模态理解基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 单一离散视觉分词器结合并行位预测和扩散解码的统一框架，能够有效弥合视觉理解与生成之间的鸿沟，实现真正的统一多模态建模。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Modeling aims to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single system. However, existing approaches typically rely on two disparate visual tokenizers, which splits the representation space and hinders truly unified modeling. We propose UniAR, a unified autoregressive framework where a single discrete visual tokenizer serves as the key bridge between understanding and generation, enabling a shared context in which the model can directly interpret its own generated visual tokens without additional re-encoding. UniAR adapts a pretrained vision encoder with multi-level feature fusion and a lookup-free bitwise quantization scheme, preserving both high-level semantics and low-level details while scaling the effective visual vocabulary at minimal cost. Building on this, the unified autoregressive model adopts parallel-bitwise-prediction to jointly predict spatially grouped, multi-level visual codes, substantially reducing visual sequence length and accelerating generation. Finally, a diffusion-based visual decoder operates on discrete visual tokens to decode high-fidelity images. Through large-scale pre-training, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, UniAR achieves state-of-the-art performance on image generation and image editing while remaining competitive on multimodal understanding benchmarks. The project page is available at https://sharelab-sii.github.io/uniar-web.

</details>


### [89] [Future Dynamic 3D Reconstruction: A 3D World Model with Disentangled Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18250)
*Nils Morbitzer,Jonathan Evers,Artem Savkin,Thomas Stauner,Nassir Navab,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FR3D是一种面向未来动态3D重建的世界模型，通过将场景3D演化与智能体轨迹解耦，并利用知识蒸馏策略，实现了从单目观测预测未来2秒内的几何一致动态3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型在2D视频合成中虽能实现高逼真度，但存在物理不一致性（如物体变形或消失），尤其是在长时域预测中。这是由于自运动与世界运动在图像平面内混合所致。

Method: 提出FR3D框架，核心包括：(1) 显式解耦场景的3D演化与智能体轨迹，将推断出的自运动作为动作的潜在代理；(2) 引入师生蒸馏策略，利用现成基础模型的空间常识实现零样本泛化。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FR3D能够从单目观测中实现未来动态3D重建，即使预测未来2秒仍保持优异性能，展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦自运动与世界运动，FR3D确保了预测的几何一致性，有望成为更可靠的动态环境预测世界模型。

Abstract: Forecasting the evolution of dynamic environments is crucial for autonomous agents. While generative world models have recently achieved high photorealism in 2D video synthesis by mixing ego-motion and environmental dynamics within the image plane, they exhibit physical inconsistencies, such as morphing or vanishing objects, especially over long time horizons. In this paper, we propose FR3D, a world model that predicts a persistent 3D latent representation for future dynamic 3D reconstruction. Unlike prior works that treat the world as a sequence of image-based features, FR3D explicitly decouples the 3D evolution of the scene from the agent's trajectory, treating the inferred ego-motion as a latent proxy for action. This disentanglement resolves the ambiguities between self-motion and world-motion, ensuring geometric consistency into the future. Furthermore, we introduce a teacher-student distillation strategy that leverages the spatial "common sense" of off-the-shelf foundation models, leading to robust zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate FR3D's strong performance for future dynamic 3D reconstruction from monocular observations across multiple datasets, even 2 seconds into the future. Project page: https://fr3d-wm.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Beyond Parallel Sampling: Diverse Query Initialization for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17209)
*Sidhaarth Murali,João Coelho,Jingjie Ning,João Magalhães,Bruno Martins,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DivInit方法，通过在第一轮生成多样化初始查询来提升智能体搜索的广度扩展效果，解决了标准并行采样中查询冗余导致的收益递减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准并行广度扩展（增加并行rollout数量）在智能体搜索中收益递减，原因是不同rollout在第一轮会生成相似的初始查询，导致检索到重叠的证据，后续轮次基于共享检索结果进行，限制了多样性。

Method: DivInit是一种免训练干预方法，作用于搜索的第一轮。具体来说，先从单次调用中生成n个候选查询，然后从中选取k个多样性最高的种子查询（k < n），将这些多样化查询作为并行轨迹独立运行。

Result: 在5个开源模型和8个基准测试上，DivInit在相同计算量下持续优于标准并行采样，在多跳问答任务上平均提升5-7个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过在第一轮引入查询多样性，可以显著提升智能体搜索的广度扩展效率，且无需额外训练，是一种简单有效的即插即用方法。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for agentic search typically increases depth (i.e., more turns and tokens per trajectory) or breadth (i.e., more parallel rollouts). Here we focus on breadth scaling, showing that standard parallel sampling yields diminishing returns, tracing this to query redundancy at the first turn. When models issue similar first queries across rollouts, the threads retrieve overlapping evidence, and subsequent turns are conditioned on this shared retrieval. We address this limitation with DivInit, a training-free intervention at the first turn. Rather than sampling k independent first queries, DivInit draws n candidates from a single call, picks k < n diverse seeds, and runs them as parallel trajectories. Across five open-weight models and eight benchmarks, DivInit consistently improves over standard parallel sampling, with average gains of five to seven points on multi-hop QA at matched compute. Code available at https://github.com/cxcscmu/diverse-query-initialization

</details>


### [91] [When Rules Learn: A Self-Evolving Agent for Legal Case Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17220)
*Mingxu Tao,Jiawei Hu,Xian Zhou,Wenpeng Hu,Jiajun Cheng,Yunbo Cao,Zhunchen Luo,Guotong Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需参数训练的自演化框架，通过LLM代理迭代生成、验证和消除查询重写规则，显著提升BM25在中文法律案例检索中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稠密检索模型在法律案例检索中取得了进展，但BM25仍然是强基线。为了在不进行参数训练的前提下增强BM25的检索能力，需要一种自动化的查询重写方法。

Method: 基于LLM的自演化框架，包含自动评估环境：LLM代理迭代创建重写规则、设计规则组合验证实验、并根据历史反馈消除无效规则，逐步优化规则集。

Result: 在LeCaRD-v2基准上，该方法优于非演化基线（包括人工设计规则和贪心规则选择），尤其在使用高容量核心LLM时效果更显著。

Conclusion: LLM利用先前实验结果的能力及其内在的规则消除知识，在通过自演化精炼规则集过程中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Legal case retrieval remains challenging due to the complexity of legal language and the need for precise lexical alignment between queries and relevant cases. Although dense retrieval models have achieved notable progress, empirical studies show that BM25 continues to serve as a strong baseline in this domain. It motivates us to propose a self-evolving framework for rule-driven query rewriting that enhances BM25 without any parameter training. The framework equips an LLM-based agent with an automatic evaluation environment, enabling it to iteratively create rewriting rules, plan validation experiments over rule combinations, and eliminate ineffective rules based on historical feedbacks. We evaluate our method on the Chinese legal case retrieval benchmark LeCaRD-v2. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms non-evolutionary baselines, including human-designed rules and greedy rule selection, particularly when powered by a highcapacity core LLM. We also conduct detailed analyses to investigate the mechanisms underlying self-evolution. Our findings reveal that LLM's capabilities to leverage previous experimental results and its intrinsic knowledge of rule elimination play critical roles in refining the rule set via self-evolution.

</details>


### [92] [SkillChain-Gym: A Benchmark for Reskilling-Aware Production-Inventory Control under Disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17266)
*Carlos Eduardo Sanoja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillChain-Gym基准规范，用于评估考虑技能再培训的生产库存控制策略，涵盖工人技能动态、认证遗忘和培训约束，实验表明无单一策略在所有场景中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运营基准通常将劳动力视为外生变量，而考虑技能与学习的劳动力规划模型很少以可复用的测试平台发布，无法满足生产规划中技能认证失效、新产品技能需求、再培训与生产竞争工时等现实需求。

Method: 设计SkillChain-Gym基准，包含单站点环境、工人技能状态动态、硬阈值认证、遗忘机制、消耗工时的培训动作；设置三类可行性模式及投影诊断、确定性重放；提出多种度量指标；评估生产-only、反应式自适应、水填充自适应、静态保险策略及其预算变体，采用60班次时域配对统计检验。

Result: 结果具有体制依赖性而非单一排序：培训能力策略优于纯生产基线，遗忘存在时维护性培训不可或缺；自适应培训在瓶颈可见于预测时有效，而精炼的静态交叉培训计划在意外冲击和缺勤下表现强劲；容量松弛和遗忘率决定两种体制的边界，无策略类跨体制占优。

Conclusion: 无单一策略类在所有场景中占优，这激励了能够决定何时购买技能保险（静态交叉培训）与何时采取反应式行动（自适应培训）的基于预测的控制器设计。

Abstract: Production planning increasingly has to treat workforce capability as a decision variable: certifications lapse when skills are not maintained, new products require skills the current workforce does not hold, and reskilling competes for the same worker hours needed for production. Existing operations benchmarks usually treat labor as exogenous, while workforce-planning models with skills and learning are rarely released as reusable testbeds. We introduce SkillChain-Gym, a benchmark specification for reskilling-aware production-inventory control: a single-site environment with stylized worker skill-state dynamics, hard threshold certification, forgetting, and capacity-consuming training actions constrained by the same per-worker time budget as production. The benchmark includes seed-controlled disruption scenarios, three feasibility modes with projection diagnostics, deterministic replay, and metrics covering operations, resilience, capability growth, and training-access distribution. We evaluate production-only, reactive adaptive, water-filling adaptive, and static-insurance policies with budget variants over 60-shift horizons with paired statistical tests. The results are regime-dependent rather than a ranking. Training-capable policies dominate the production-only baseline, and maintenance training is necessary under forgetting even without disruptions. Among training-capable classes, adaptive training helps when bottlenecks are visible in the forecast, while a lean static cross-training plan, a deliberately favorable comparator whose structure encodes relevant skill contingencies, acts as strong insurance under surprise shocks and absenteeism. Capacity slack and the forgetting rate govern the boundary between these regimes. No policy class dominates across regimes, motivating forecast-driven controllers that decide when to buy skill insurance and when to react.

</details>


### [93] [Skill-Constrained Model Predictive Control for Resilient Manufacturing Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17269)
*Carlos Eduardo Sanoja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究技能约束的生产-库存系统中，模型预测控制器与静态策略的对比。通过SkillChain-Gym场景评估发现：没有策略类别占优，预测控制仅在技能瓶颈可提前预测时有效，而静态保险策略在意外冲击下表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在技能约束的生产-库存系统中，培训决策影响未来合格人力容量，而培训与生产争抢当前稀缺工时。需要研究模型预测控制在这种闭环系统中的表现。

Method: 提出闭环技能约束模型预测控制器，每班次求解有限时域混合整数规划（涉及生产、库存、积压、培训），含二元预测认证、硬生产资格约束和可解释终端价值。在合成SkillChain-Gym场景中，与生产-only、维护-only消融、静态交叉培训保险计划和强反应式启发式进行对比评估。

Result: 没有策略类别占优，存在体制依赖性。预测控制仅在技能或劳动力瓶颈可提前足够预测时有效；静态保险在意外冲击、需求-产能边界附近和冲击前松弛使保险成本低的情况下表现优异。

Conclusion: 预测性而非适应性本身决定了预测控制的回报。归因消融区分了认证维护、过期认证重新获取和新技能获取三种场景。

Abstract: In skill-constrained production-inventory systems, the qualified human capacity available tomorrow depends on training decisions made today: production requires certified workers, certifications decay unless maintained, and training consumes the same scarce worker hours that production needs now. We study a closed-loop skill-constrained model predictive controller that, at every shift, solves a finite-horizon mixed-integer program over production, inventory, backlog, and training, with binary predicted certification, hard production eligibility, and an interpretable terminal value that prices certified-capacity gaps at the horizon boundary; only the first-period action is applied before replanning. On synthetic, seed-controlled SkillChain-Gym scenarios - announced and surprise new-skill shocks, demand shocks, absenteeism, forecast- and availability-quality modes, capacity-boundary and training-rate sweeps, and negative controls - we evaluate the controller against production-only and maintenance-only ablations, static cross-training insurance plans, and a strong reactive heuristic, under an ex-ante locked configuration and paired statistics. The result is regime dependence, not superiority: no policy class dominates. Predictive control helps when skill or labor bottlenecks are forecastable early enough for training to complete; lean static insurance remains hard to beat under surprise shocks, near the demand-capacity boundary, and wherever pre-shock slack makes insurance cheap. Attribution ablations separate certification maintenance, re-acquisition of lapsed certifications, and greenfield skill acquisition. Forecastability, not adaptivity per se, decides when predictive control pays.

</details>


### [94] [Nothing from Something: Can a Language Model Discover 0?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17289)
*Phoebe Zeng,Thomas L. Griffiths,Brenden M. Lake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI系统能否在数学领域实现超越训练数据的泛化，以算术中的"零"概念为案例，发现GPT-2规模的语言模型无法在测试时独立发现零的概念，但通过少量示例训练后可以改进，且语言预训练能减少约50%所需示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于人工神经网络的AI系统能否超越训练数据边界，实现数学发现所需的强分布外泛化能力，特别是验证语言能力是否如人类认知中那样支持这种数学泛化。

Method: 以简单算术中的"零"概念发现为案例，使用GPT-2规模的语言模型，对比有无语言预训练的情况，评估模型在测试时能否独立发现零的概念，以及通过少量示例训练后的改进效果。

Result: (1) GPT-2规模的语言模型无论是否经过语言预训练，均无法在测试时独立泛化出零的概念；(2) 通过数十到数百个零的示例训练后，模型性能显著提升；(3) 语言预训练可将所需示例数量减少约50%。

Conclusion: 语言能力可以在神经模型中支撑数学发现，但当前小规模模型在测试时尚未具备完全自主的数学概念创新能力，需要借助少量示例引导。

Abstract: AI systems based on artificial neural networks are being developed with aspirations of pushing the boundary of human mathematical knowledge. A key question for these systems is how much they can reach beyond their training data. Mathematical discovery requires a strong form of out of distribution generalization; the ability to hypothesize genuinely new - and potentially logically more powerful - mathematical structures. It has been hypothesized that language abilities support such generalizations in human cognition. In this work, we use simple arithmetic as a case study for examining how modern AI models could expand their mathematical horizons, evaluating whether these models can independently discover the concept of "zero". We show that We show that (1) language models of a GPT-2 size are unable to perform this generalization at test time regardless of language pretraining, but (2) models can improve substantially after training on tens or hundreds of examples of zero. Additionally, we find that language pretraining reduces the number of required examples by approximately $50\%$, showing that language abilities can scaffold mathematical discovery in neural models.

</details>


### [95] [Quantifying Consistency in LLM Logical Reasoning via Structural Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17312)
*Baishali Chaudhury,Mengdie Flora Wang,Hyunji Hayley Park,Rahul Ghosh,Sungmin Hong,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构不确定性框架，通过自偏好排序的稳定性评估大模型推理一致性，将其分解为跨试验排名不稳定性和试验内候选歧义两个熵分量，在逻辑/数学推理任务中与答案离散度互补，在事实检索任务中失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖输出离散度（答案差异）评估大模型推理可靠性，但忽略了模型能否一致地排序竞争性推理候选路径这一互补信号，尤其是在多步演绎推理中模型常通过不稳定、矛盾或难以一致排名的路径得出相同答案。

Method: 首先生成多个候选推理方案，让模型对其自身输出进行成对偏好判断；通过Bradley-Terry模型与PageRank聚合自偏好形成排序分布；将信号分解为两个熵分量：跨试验排名不稳定性和试验内候选歧义。

Result: 在5个LLM和8个基准上，结构信号与答案离散度提供互补信息；逻辑和数学推理任务中组合使用可改善不可靠实例的识别；事实检索任务中结构信号坍缩为均匀分布；试验内歧义与正确性正相关，跨试验不稳定性与正确性负相关。

Conclusion: 结构不确定性应被视为一种区域敏感的推理一致性评估器，而非通用的置信度估计器；它提供了比单纯答案离散度更丰富的推理可靠性信号，但在不同任务类型（推理型vs事实型）中表现存在本质差异。

Abstract: Large language models can arrive at the same answer through reasoning paths that are unstable, contradictory, or difficult to rank consistently -- a failure mode especially prevalent in multi-step deductive reasoning. Existing methods assess reliability primarily through output dispersion -- measuring how much sampled answers differ -- but this discards a complementary signal: whether the model can consistently rank competing reasoning candidates. We propose structural uncertainty, a consistency-aware framework derived from the stability of self-preference-induced rankings over sampled reasoning solutions. Given a query, we generate multiple candidate solutions and ask the model to judge pairwise preferences among its own outputs. We aggregate self-preferences into ranking distributions via Bradley-Terry modeling with PageRank, and decompose the signal into two entropy-based components: across-trial ranking instability and within-trial candidate ambiguity. Across five LLMs and eight benchmarks, structural signals provide information complementary to answer dispersion: on logical and mathematical reasoning tasks, the combination improves identification of unreliable instances, while on factual retrieval the structural signal collapses toward uniformity, diagnosing a regime boundary where reasoning-level consistency evaluation is uninformative. The two components relate differently to accuracy: within-trial ambiguity correlates positively with correctness -- consistent with settings where multiple plausible solution paths remain competitive -- while across-trial instability correlates negatively, signaling unreliable reasoning. Structural uncertainty is best understood not as a universal confidence estimator, but as a regime-sensitive evaluator of logical reasoning consistency.

</details>


### [96] [MemTrace: Probing What Final Accuracy Misses in Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17328)
*Xianxuan Long,Zhikai Chen,Shenglai Zeng,Shouren Wang,Kai Guo,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemTrace是一个以知识点（而非单个问题）为评估单元的基准，通过三个维度（记忆年龄、问题类型、证据条件）系统探测长期记忆，发现类似的总准确率掩盖了不同的失败模式，主要瓶颈是证据利用而非检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆评估通常按问题行聚合准确率，无法揭示同一事实在不同条件变化下的行为表现。

Method: 提出MemTrace基准，以知识点为评估单元，沿记忆年龄（会话间隔）、问题类型（当前状态/历史状态/变化轨迹）、证据条件（存在/缺失/错误前提矛盾）三个维度系统评估长期记忆系统。

Result: 评估13种记忆系统配置后发现：相似的总准确率掩盖了不同失败模式；恢复事实的当前和历史状态不意味着能追踪变化；安全弃权不意味着能纠正错误前提；主要瓶颈是证据利用（90%的失败中证据可检索但未被使用），而非检索或存储。

Conclusion: 改善长期记忆需要更好地利用可获取的证据，而非简单地增加存储或提升检索能力。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly maintain long-term memory of user facts across sessions. Yet such memory is usually evaluated by aggregating accuracy over question rows or episodes. Because this approach scores question rows independently, even when several questions probe the same fact, it cannot show how that fact behaves as conditions change. We introduce MemTrace, a benchmark whose unit of measurement is the knowledge point: a single typed fact about the user, rather than an individual question. MemTrace probes each fact along three controlled dimensions: memory age, defined by how many sessions ago the fact appeared in the history; question type, covering current state, earlier state, and trajectory of change; and evidence condition, covering present, missing, and contradicted-by-false-premise settings. Evaluating 13 memory-system configurations across four paradigms, we find that similar pooled accuracy hides different failures: recovering a fact's current and earlier states does not imply tracking how it changed, and safe abstention does not imply correcting a false premise. The dominant bottleneck is evidence use, not retrieval: when systems fail, the evidence was retrievable 10 times more often than it was missing. These results suggest that improving long-term memory requires better use of reachable evidence, not simply more storage or retrieval.

</details>


### [97] [SpeechDx: A Multi-Task Benchmark for Clinical Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17339)
*Sejal Bhalla,Larry Kieu,Aina Merchant,Eyal de Lara,Alex Mariakakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpeechDx是一个涵盖12个数据集、27个任务的临床语音AI大规模基准测试，通过按语音产生阶段（概念化、形成、发音）对任务进行分类来评估模型泛化能力。研究发现大规模语音模型表现最佳，但尚无模型能在临床语音领域可靠泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床语音AI方法多局限于孤立的特定疾病研究，导致结果难以比较、泛化能力难以评估，缺乏统一的评价框架。

Method: 构建包含12个数据集、27个任务的SpeechDx基准；按语音产生阶段（概念化、形成、发音）组织任务；评估12种先进音频编码器在所有任务上的表现，包括零样本跨条件迁移。

Result: 大规模语音模型是最强基线；领域专用模型仅在高度匹配的任务上提升性能；尚无当前表示能在临床语音领域可靠泛化。

Conclusion: SpeechDx为追踪通用临床语音表示的研究进展建立了共享评估框架。

Abstract: Speech offers a uniquely informative window into health by simultaneously engaging neurological, motor, respiratory, and vocal systems. Current clinical speech AI methods have largely progressed through isolated condition-specific studies, making results difficult to compare and generalization difficult to assess. We introduce SpeechDx, a large-scale benchmark for clinical speech AI spanning 12 datasets and 27 tasks across diverse health conditions. To enable evaluation across shared clinical mechanisms, SpeechDx structures tasks by the stage of speech production they disrupt: conceptualization, formulation, and articulation. The benchmark tests generalization by including tasks with limited labeled data and evaluating the same health condition across multiple datasets, distinguishing clinically meaningful patterns from dataset artefacts. We systematically evaluate 12 state-of-the-art audio encoders across all tasks and under zero-shot cross-condition transfer. Results show that large-scale speech models represent the strongest overall baselines, domain-specific models improve performance only on closely matched tasks, and no current representation generalizes reliably across the clinical speech landscape. SpeechDx establishes a shared evaluation framework for tracking progress toward general-purpose clinical speech representations

</details>


### [98] [Distributed General-Purpose Agent Networks: Architecture, Key Mechanisms, and Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17368)
*Shengli Zhang,Deen Ma,Zibin Lin,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式通用智能体网络，提出分层架构解决开放P2P环境下异构智能体的发现、信任建立与合作执行问题，并针对语义公告传播、多方信誉治理和开放任务执行三类核心机制给出技术路线与原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个智能体受限于本地数据、工具权限、运行时环境和治理边界，无法独立完成跨域复杂任务。现有P2P网络与多智能体系统的简单组合无法满足智能体网络对语义声明传播、信任协商和开放任务执行的需求。

Method: 提出以协议适配层为核心的分层架构，连接上层任务语义与下层网络操作；针对三个核心机制分别给出具体方案：基于无体八卦与顺序日志的语义公告传播、基于BAID身份绑定与MG-EigenTrust声誉的多主题信誉治理、以及基于Stackelberg博弈与语义归因反馈的机制生成循环。

Result: 报告了BAID分层验证的原型开销结果，以及跨主题伪装-合谋攻击下MG-EigenTrust的机制级仿真结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为开放、可信、可扩展的智能体协作提供了系统级基础。

Abstract: Large language models have accelerated the transition from passive conversational assistants to autonomous agents that can understand goals, plan actions, invoke tools, and execute multi-step tasks. Yet the capability of a single agent remains constrained by its local data, tool permissions, runtime environment, and governance boundary. This paper studies distributed general-purpose agent networks: open peer-to-peer networks in which heterogeneous agents deployed on personal devices, edge nodes, or autonomous computing environments can discover one another, establish trust, negotiate cooperation rules, and execute open-ended tasks. We argue that such networks cannot be obtained by simply combining existing peer-to-peer overlays with conventional multi-agent systems. Unlike traditional P2P networks, agent networks must propagate semantic declarations about intentions, capabilities, states, and cooperation constraints. We therefore propose a layered architecture centered on a protocol adaptation layer that connects upper-level task semantics with lower-level network operations. Based on this architecture, the paper identifies three core mechanism problems: semantic announcement propagation for collaborator discovery, verifiable identity and multi-topic reputation for cooperation governance, and semantic-gradient mechanism design for open task execution. For each problem, we present a technical route, including bodyless gossip with sequential logs, BAID-based identity binding with MG-EigenTrust reputation, and a Stackelberg-style mechanism-generation loop driven by semantic attribution feedback. We further report prototype overhead results for BAID-style tiered verification and mechanism-level simulations of MG-EigenTrust under cross-topic disguise-collusion attacks. The resulting framework provides a system-level foundation for open, trustworthy, and scalable agent collaboration.

</details>


### [99] [Treatment Response Optimized Clinical Decision Support AI System via Digital Twin Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17405)
*Xinyu Qin,Anil K. Sood,Ruiheng Yu,Sara Corvigno,Elaine Stur,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线自适应临床决策支持AI框架，结合治疗效果估计、数字孪生和强化学习，通过规则模块保障安全，并在合成数据和真实卵巢癌数据集上验证了其有效性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持AI系统需要实时适应不断变化的患者状况，同时严格遵守安全约束，现有方法在动态环境中的适应性和安全性难以兼顾。

Method: 框架包含三部分：1）治疗效果（TE）估计量化临床获益；2）患者数字孪生（DT）模拟治疗轨迹；3）强化学习（RL）进行序贯决策。系统先以历史数据预训练，持续在线学习；规则模块监测生命体征阻止禁忌治疗；模型内部分歧大的案例交由临床专家审核。

Result: 在合成临床模拟器和TCGA卵巢癌真实数据集上，该方法在推荐治疗的有效性和稳定性上均优于标准计算基线；系统延迟低，仅少数案例需要专家介入。

Conclusion: 该框架具有作为安全、临床医生监督下的个性化医疗工具的潜力，能够在实际使用中持续改进。

Abstract: Clinical decision support AI systems (CDSASs) must adapt to evolving patient conditions in real-time while adhering to strict safety constraints. We present an online adaptive framework that integrates Treatment Effect (TE) estimation to quantify clinical benefits, a patient Digital Twin (DT) to simulate treatment trajectories, and Reinforcement Learning (RL) for sequential decision-making. The AI system is initially trained on historical medical records and operates in a continuous learning loop. To ensure safety, a rule-based module monitors vital signs and blocks contraindicated treatments. Cases with strong internal model disagreement are flagged for clinician review, simulated in our experiments via a pre-trained outcome model. We validate our framework using both a synthetic clinical simulator and a real-world ovarian cancer dataset from The Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA). In both simulated and clinical settings, our method demonstrated superior effectiveness and stability in recommending treatments compared to standard computational baselines. Furthermore, the AI system maintains low latency and requires expert consultation for only a minority of cases in our experimental validation, demonstrating its potential as a safe, clinician-supervised tool for personalized medicine that continuously improves through practical use.

</details>


### [100] [Incumbent Advantage: Brand Bias and Cognitive Manipulation Dynamics in LLM Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17443)
*Xi Chu,Yupeng Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了LLM推荐场景下的品牌竞争动态，发现知名品牌在规格相同时形成"条件垄断"，但微小评分优势即可打破；权威营销语言（含虚假临床证据）能以+0.17评分点的偏差价值打破垄断；多品牌GEO竞争存在社会困境，个体采用相同优化策略时收益从+0.802降至+0.007，非参与品牌推荐量为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正成为消费者发现产品的主要渠道，但品牌如何在这一新渠道中竞争尚未被理解。本文以护肤品（经验品）为研究对象，探究LLM推荐中的品牌动态。

Method: 使用三种商业LLM（GPT-4o-mini、Claude Sonnet、Gemini 3 Flash）进行三项实验，以护肤品为经验品类别，并用搜索品进行稳健性检验。

Result: (1)规格相同时知名品牌100%被推荐（IAI=10.0），但竞争对手仅需+0.1星优势即可打破垄断；(2)权威营销语言（含虚假临床证据）以+0.17评分点的偏差价值打破垄断，不同模型反应各异；(3)多品牌GEO竞争中，所有品牌采用相同优化策略时个体收益从+0.802降至+0.007，非参与品牌推荐量为零。

Conclusion: 生成式引擎优化（GEO）不仅是安全风险，也应作为影响市场竞争的新兴营销实践加以研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming a major way for consumers to find products, but we do not yet understand how brands compete in this new channel. We study brand dynamics in LLM recommendations using skincare products -- a category where consumers cannot easily judge quality before buying and must rely on brand reputation -- across three commercial LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, Claude Sonnet, Gemini 3 Flash), with a robustness check on search goods. In three experiments, we find: (1) a Conditional Monopoly where well-known brands get recommended 100% of the time (IAI = 10.0) when all products have the same specifications, but this dominance disappears with less than a +0.1-star rating advantage for a competitor; (2) authority-style marketing language, including fabricated clinical-evidence claims, breaks this monopoly at a Bias Surplus Value equal to +0.17 rating points, with each model responding differently; and (3) a social dilemma in multi-brand GEO competition: when all brands adopt the same optimization strategy, individual payoff falls from +0.802 to +0.007 in our payoff proxy, and non-participating brands receive zero recommendations in our tests. Our results suggest that generative engine optimization (GEO) should be studied not only as a security risk, but also as an emerging marketing practice that shapes market competition.

</details>


### [101] [A Machine-Learned Comorbidity Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17450)
*Suleman Baloch,Kishlay Jha,Alberto M. Segre,Philip M. Polgreen,Bijaya Adhikari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的共病指数（MLCI），通过最大化标准化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（nHSIC）将诊断代码映射为单一标量，克服了传统共病评分（如Charlson和Elixhauser指数）以死亡率为中心、线性规则结构无法捕捉非线性风险关系的局限性。MLCI能够捕捉非线性风险-结局依赖关系，并在多个电子健康记录数据集上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共病评分（Charlson、Elixhauser等）存在两个关键局限：一是主要针对死亡率设计，与其他临床结局（如再入院率、并发症等）的对齐性差；二是其线性的、基于规则的简单结构无法捕捉非线性的、结局特异性的风险关系。因此需要一种能自适应学习、支持多结局的共病评估方法。

Method: 提出机器学习共病指数（MLCI），通过最大化标准化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（nHSIC）将诊断代码学习映射为一个标量分数。nHSIC能够衡量学习到的分数与多种临床结局之间的非线性依赖关系，从而确保所得分数对多个结局都具有信息量。理论部分刻画了能够在多个结局上实现统一的、有信息量的入院级别排序的条件。

Result: 在多个基准电子健康记录（EHR）数据集上，MLCI在多种评估指标上均优于包括Charlson和Elixhauser在内的强基线方法，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLCI作为一种数据驱动的、多结局对齐的共病指数，有效克服了传统评分的局限性，为风险调整和患者分层提供了更灵活、更准确的工具。

Abstract: Traditional comorbidity scores (e.g., Charlson and Elixhauser) are widely used for risk adjustment and patient stratification, but they have two key limitations: (i) they are largely mortality-centric and do not align well with other clinical outcomes, and (ii) their linear, rule-based structure cannot capture nonlinear, outcome-specific risk relationships. We propose a Machine-Learned Comorbidity Index (MLCI) that maps diagnosis codes to a single scalar by maximizing the normalized Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (nHSIC) between the learned score and multiple clinical outcomes. MLCI captures nonlinear risk-outcome dependence and is supported by a theory that characterizes when a unified, informative admission-level ordering can be achieved across outcomes. Empirical results on multiple benchmark electronic health record (EHR) datasets show that MLCI outperforms strong baselines across multiple evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [102] [MapSatisfyBench: Benchmarking Satisfaction-Aware Map Agents through Behavior-Grounded Implicit Decision Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17453)
*Lubin Bai,Mengyu Cao,Sixue Wang,Zhongwei Wan,Yue Pan,Jiale Hou,Xiang Li,Xiuyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MapSatisfyBench基准，用于评估地图语言模型代理在隐含决策因素上的用户满意度感知能力，发现当前代理在显式任务完成上表现良好，但在满足隐式需求方面存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在地图服务中的查询常存在未言明的隐含决策因素（implicit decision factors），这些因素对用户满意度至关重要。虽然澄清可以缓解此问题，但会增加用户负担，因此代理应主动从可用信息源恢复这些因素。然而，评估这种能力面临两个挑战：确定哪些隐含因素是可评估的，以及用户满意度无法由单一参考答案可靠表示。

Method: 提出restore-identify-filter框架，从行为链证据中重构完整用户需求，识别隐含决策因素，并仅保留查询前证据支持的因素。基于此方法，从大规模真实匿名用户数据构建MapSatisfyBench，从五个维度标注真实标签，实现满意度感知地图代理的全链路评估。

Result: 实验表明当前代理在显式任务完成上表现良好，但在满足隐含决策因素和主动获取满意度感知决策所需证据方面仍存在局限。

Conclusion: MapSatisfyBench将地图代理评估从任务完成转向满意度感知的空间决策，推动了评估范式的转变。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly integrated into map services. Since map services are embedded in everyday-life scenarios rather than professional task settings, users often express their needs informally, resulting in underspecified queries with many unspoken needs, namely, implicit decision factors that are critical for user satisfaction. Although clarification is an effective way to mitigate this issue, it increases user burden in daily interaction, and a capable agent should first proactively recover such factors from available information sources. However, evaluating this ability is challenging. The first challenge is to determine which implicit decision factors are suitable for evaluation. A factor is evaluable only if it affects user acceptance and can be recovered from information available to the agent before it responds. Second, user satisfaction cannot be reliably represented by a single reference answer, requiring a benchmark that converts satisfaction-relevant factors into objective and quantifiable evaluation targets. To address these challenges, we propose a restore-identify-filter framework that reconstructs complete user needs from behavior-chain evidence, identifies implicit decision factors, and retains only those supported by pre-query evidence. Building on this methodology, we construct MapSatisfyBench from large-scale, real-world anonymized user data and annotate ground truth from five dimensions and enables full-chain evaluation of satisfaction-aware map agents. Experiments show that current agents generally perform well on explicit task completion, but remain limited in satisfying implicit decision factors and proactively acquiring the evidence needed for satisfaction-aware decisions. These findings establish MapSatisfyBench as a benchmark for shifting map-agent evaluation from task completion toward satisfaction-aware spatial decision making.

</details>


### [103] [Dissecting model behavior through agent trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17454)
*Gaurav Gupta,Vatshank Chaturvedi,Jun Huan,Anoop Deoras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI智能体性能本质上是一个系统问题，形式化了"意图-执行差距"概念，并开发了Simple Strands Agent (SSA)框架来弥合模型与执行环境之间的对齐问题。SSA在多个基准测试上实现或超越了现有最佳性能，并通过分析138k轨迹揭示了不同模型在问题解决行为上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体研究过度关注模型能力本身，忽视了模型与执行环境之间的对齐问题。模型的能力需要通过"智能体框架"（agent harness）来实现，而模型假设与框架行为之间的不匹配会阻碍模型能力的充分发挥。作者希望正式化这一问题并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出了Simple Strands Agent (SSA)框架，该框架通过识别跨不同模型家族（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok、Qwen）的通用模式，以及少量模型特定的偏好，来最小化意图-执行差距。同时，通过在代码状态空间中表示智能体轨迹，对138k轨迹进行细粒度分析。

Result: SSA在SWE-Pro、SWE-Verified和Terminal-Bench-2等主流智能体基准测试上复现或超越了不同模型提供家族报告的pass@1性能。通过细粒度指标（如编辑频率、测试活动、阶段转换）的分析，揭示了不同前沿模型在问题求解行为上的差异，而这些差异在pass@1指标上并不明显。

Conclusion: 最小化意图-执行差距与工具和执行循环等其他框架设计同样重要。通过合理的框架-模型对齐设计，可以更好地发挥模型能力。同时，细粒度行为分析比单纯的pass@1指标更能揭示不同模型在实际问题求解中的差异。

Abstract: AI agent performance is not just a modeling problem, it is fundamentally a systems problem. The advanced capabilities of models are realized through agent harnesses. Therefore, a gap between model assumptions and harness behavior can easily prevent the model's full capabilities from translating into agent performance.
